    <script>
    (function(){
        var ACCENT={
            amber:  ['#c4622d','#d4722d','rgba(196,98,45,0.08)','rgba(196,98,45,0.18)'],
            cyan:   ['#00b4d8','#00c8f0','rgba(0,180,216,0.08)','rgba(0,180,216,0.18)'],
            emerald:['#22c55e','#16a34a','rgba(34,197,94,0.08)','rgba(34,197,94,0.18)'],
            violet: ['#8b5cf6','#7c3aed','rgba(139,92,246,0.08)','rgba(139,92,246,0.18)'],
            white:  ['#d4d0cb','#e4e0db','rgba(212,208,203,0.06)','rgba(212,208,203,0.14)']
        };
        try {
            var el = document.documentElement;
            // 1. Accent color
            var ac = localStorage.getItem('ce_accent') || 'amber';
            var c  = ACCENT[ac] || ACCENT.amber;
            el.style.setProperty('--accent',       c[0]);
            el.style.setProperty('--accent-hover', c[1]);
            el.style.setProperty('--accent-glow',  c[2]);
            el.style.setProperty('--accent-glow-2',c[3]);
            // 2. Tema
            var th = localStorage.getItem('ce_theme') || 'auto';
            var ts = localStorage.getItem('ce_theme_sync') || 'manual';
            var isDark = (ts==='auto')
                ? window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
                : true;
            var appliedTheme = th;
            if(ts==='auto') appliedTheme = isDark ? 'dark' : 'dark'; // app dark-first
            if(appliedTheme==='amoled'){
                el.style.setProperty('--bg','#000000');
                el.style.setProperty('--surface','#0a0a0a');
                el.style.setProperty('--surface-2','#0f0f0f');
            }
            // 3. Density
            var dn = localStorage.getItem('ce_density') || 'comfortable';
            el.dataset.density = dn;
        } catch(e){}
    })();
    </script>

function _achGet()       { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('nl_ach') || '[]'); } catch { return []; } }
function _achMark(id)    { const l = _achGet(); if (!l.includes(id)) { l.push(id); localStorage.setItem('nl_ach', JSON.stringify(l)); } }
function _achCount(key)  { return parseInt(localStorage.getItem(key) || '0'); }
function _achBump(key)   { const n = _achCount(key) + 1; localStorage.setItem(key, n); return n; }

function _checkAchievements(type, count) {
    const unlocked = _achGet();
    _ACHIEVEMENTS
        .filter(a => a.type === type && a.n <= count && !unlocked.includes(a.id))
        .forEach(a => { _achMark(a.id); _showAchievement(a); });
}

function _showAchievement(ach) {
    const tc = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
    if (!tc) return;

    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.style.cssText = 'pointer-events:auto;max-width:310px;animation:toastIn .35s var(--ease) forwards;will-change:transform,opacity';
    el.innerHTML = `
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(196,98,45,0.14),rgba(196,98,45,0.04));
                    border:1px solid rgba(196,98,45,0.4);border-radius:var(--radius-sm);
                    padding:16px 18px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;
                    box-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.55),0 0 0 1px rgba(196,98,45,0.08)">
            <div style="font-size:30px;line-height:1;flex-shrink:0">${ach.icon}</div>
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
                <div style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1.8px;
                            text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--accent);margin-bottom:4px">
                    Achievement Deblocat ✦
                </div>
                <div style="font-size:13.5px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text-primary);margin-bottom:2px">
                    ${ach.title}
                </div>
                <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text-secondary)">${ach.desc}</div>
            </div>
            <button onclick="this.closest('[style]').parentElement.remove()"
                    style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--text-muted);cursor:pointer;
                           font-size:18px;line-height:1;padding:2px 4px;flex-shrink:0;
                           transition:color .15s;"
                    onmouseover="this.style.color='var(--text-primary)'"
                    onmouseout="this.style.color='var(--text-muted)'">×</button>
        </div>`;
    tc.appendChild(el);

    setTimeout(() => {
        el.style.animation = 'toastOut .3s var(--ease) forwards';
        el.addEventListener('animationend', () => el.remove(), { once: true });
    }, 6000);
}

// ══ TOAST SYSTEM ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function toast(msg, type = 'info', duration = 3500) {
    const icons = { success:'✓', error:'✕', info:'◉' };
    const tc    = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
    if (!tc) return;

    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = `toast ${type}`;
    el.innerHTML =
        `<span class="toast-icon">${icons[type] || icons.info}</span>` +
        `<span class="toast-msg">${msg}</span>` +
        `<button class="toast-close" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()">×</button>`;
    tc.appendChild(el);

    setTimeout(() => {
        el.classList.add('removing');
        el.addEventListener('animationend', () => el.remove(), { once: true });
    }, duration);
}

// ══ COUNT-UP ANIMATION ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function countUp(el, target, duration = 700, decimals = 0, suffix = '') {
    if (!el || isNaN(target)) return;
    const start     = 0;
    const startTime = performance.now();
    const easeOut   = t => 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
    function tick(now) {
        const p   = Math.min((now - startTime) / duration, 1);
        const val = start + (target - start) * easeOut(p);
        el.textContent = val.toFixed(decimals) + suffix;
        if (p < 1) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

// ══ SKELETON HELPERS ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function skeletonFoodList(n = 3) {
    return Array.from({ length: n }, () =>
        `<div class="skeleton-card">
            <div class="skeleton-row">
                <div class="skeleton skeleton-circle"></div>
                <div style="flex:1">
                    <div class="skeleton skeleton-line lg"></div>
                    <div class="skeleton skeleton-line sm"></div>
                </div>
                <div class="skeleton skeleton-line" style="width:52px;height:24px;margin:0"></div>
            </div>
        </div>`
    ).join('');
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  INTERCEPTOR GLOBAL 401
//  Orice fetch care returnează 401 când există un token salvat = sesiune expirată.
//  Înlocuim window.fetch cu un wrapper transparent — codul existent nu se schimbă.
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
(function installFetchInterceptor() {
    const _native = window.fetch;
    window.fetch = async function(...args) {
        let response;
        try {
            response = await _native.apply(this, args);
        } catch (networkError) {
            throw networkError;
        }
        // Declanșăm handleUnauthorized doar dacă:
        //  1. statusul e 401
        //  2. există un token în storage (nu suntem pe login/signup)
        //  3. nu e un endpoint de autentificare (nu vrem loop)
        const url = (args[0] instanceof Request ? args[0].url : String(args[0])) || '';
        const isAuthEndpoint = /\/(auth\/(login|signup|validate-token|verify-email|forgot-password|reset-password))/.test(url);
        if (response.status === 401 && localStorage.getItem('coaching_token') && !isAuthEndpoint) {
            // Folosim clone() ca răspunsul original să poată fi citit în continuare de apelant
            handleUnauthorized();
        }
        return response;
    };
})();

// ── AUTH ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let currentAuthTab = 'login';
// Inițializare: login tab activ → link "Am uitat parola" vizibil
document.getElementById('forgotLinkWrap').style.display = 'block';
function switchAuthTab(tab) {
    currentAuthTab = tab;
    document.getElementById('tabBtnLogin').classList.toggle('active', tab==='login');
    document.getElementById('tabBtnSignup').classList.toggle('active', tab==='signup');
    document.getElementById('authSubmitBtn').textContent = tab==='login' ? 'Conectare' : 'Creează Cont';
    document.getElementById('authMsg').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('authPassword').value = '';
    document.getElementById('forgotLinkWrap').style.display = tab==='login' ? 'block' : 'none';
}
function showAuthMsg(text, type) {
    const msg = document.getElementById('authMsg');
    msg.textContent = text; msg.className = `auth-msg ${type}`; msg.style.display = 'block';
}
function hideAuthOverlay() {
    const o = document.getElementById('authOverlay');
    o.style.transition = 'opacity .4s var(--ease)'; o.style.opacity = '0'; o.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    setTimeout(() => { o.style.display = 'none'; }, 420);
}

// ── AUTH OVERLAY · show + unauthorized handler ────────────────────────────
function showAuthOverlay() {
    const o = document.getElementById('authOverlay');
    o.style.display    = 'flex';
    o.style.opacity    = '1';
    o.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
    o.style.transition = 'none';
}

// ── Handle ?upgrade=1 (redirect de la landing.html) ──────────────────────────
(function handleUpgradeParam() {
    if (new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('upgrade') !== '1') return;
    window.history.replaceState({}, '', '/');  // curăță URL-ul imediat

    const doCheckout = () => {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
        if (token) {
            // User logat → inițiem checkout după ce interfața s-a încărcat
            setTimeout(startStripeCheckout, 900);
        } else {
            // User nelogat → overlay de login, checkout după autentificare
            showAuthOverlay();
            showAuthMsg('Conectează-te pentru a activa Premium.', 'success');
        }
    };

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', doCheckout);
    } else {
        doCheckout();
    }
})();

/**
 * Apelat de interceptorul global 401.
 * Șterge token-ul, ascunde navigarea și reafișează overlay-ul de login.
 */
function handleUnauthorized() {
    // Evităm să declanșăm de mai multe ori în aceeași sesiune
    if (_handlingUnauthorized) return;
    _handlingUnauthorized = true;
    localStorage.removeItem('coaching_token');
    const nav = document.getElementById('sectionNav');
    if (nav) nav.style.display = 'none';
    showAuthOverlay();
    switchAuthTab('login');
    showAuthMsg('Sesiunea a expirat. Te rugăm să te reconectezi.', 'error');
    // Resetează flag-ul după ce overlay-ul e vizibil
    setTimeout(() => { _handlingUnauthorized = false; }, 3000);
}
let _handlingUnauthorized = false;
async function startStripeCheckout() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) { showToast('⚠ Trebuie să fii logat', true); return; }
    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/stripe/checkout', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (res.ok && data.checkout_url) {
            window.location.href = data.checkout_url;
        } else {
            showToast(data.detail || '⚠ Eroare la checkout', true);
        }
    } catch { showToast('⚠ Eroare de conexiune', true); }
}
// ── PASSWORD RESET ────────────────────────────────────────────
function showForgotPanel() {
    document.getElementById('authMainContent').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('forgotPanel').style.display    = 'block';
    document.getElementById('resetPanel').style.display     = 'none';
    setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('forgotEmail').focus(), 60);
}
function hideForgotPanel() {
    document.getElementById('authMainContent').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('forgotPanel').style.display    = 'none';
    document.getElementById('forgotMsg').style.display      = 'none';
    document.getElementById('forgotEmail').value            = '';
}
function _showForgotMsg(text, type) {
    const m = document.getElementById('forgotMsg');
    m.textContent = text; m.className = `auth-msg ${type}`; m.style.display = 'block';
}
function _showResetMsg(text, type) {
    const m = document.getElementById('resetMsg');
    m.textContent = text; m.className = `auth-msg ${type}`; m.style.display = 'block';
}
async function handleForgotPassword() {
    const email = document.getElementById('forgotEmail').value.trim();
    if (!email) { _showForgotMsg('Introdu adresa de email.', 'error'); return; }
    const btn = document.getElementById('forgotBtn');
    btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Se trimite...';
    document.getElementById('forgotMsg').style.display = 'none';
    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/auth/forgot-password', {
            method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({email})
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        _showForgotMsg(data.message || '✓ Email trimis!', res.ok ? 'success' : 'error');
    } catch {
        _showForgotMsg('Eroare de conexiune. Verifică că serverul rulează.', 'error');
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Trimite link';
    }
}

async function startStripeCheckoutAnnual() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/stripe/checkout-annual', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
        });
        if (!res.ok) { toast('Planul anual nu este configurat încă.', 'error'); return; }
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.checkout_url) window.location.href = data.checkout_url;
    } catch(e) {
        toast('Eroare la checkout. Încearcă din nou.', 'error');
    }
}

// ── STRIPE PORTAL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function openStripePortal() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    const btn = event?.currentTarget;
    const origText = btn?.textContent;
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Se deschide portalul…'; }
    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/stripe/portal', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token,
                       'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (res.ok && data.portal_url) {
            window.location.href = data.portal_url;
        } else {
            const msg = data.detail?.message || data.detail || '⚠ Eroare la portal Stripe';
            showToast(msg, true);
        }
    } catch {
        showToast('⚠ Eroare de conexiune', true);
    } finally {
        if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = origText; }
    }
}
async function handleResetPassword() {
    const token = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('reset_token');
    const pw    = document.getElementById('resetPassword').value;
    const pw2   = document.getElementById('resetPasswordConfirm').value;
    if (!pw || pw.length < 8) { _showResetMsg('Parola trebuie să aibă cel puțin 8 caractere.', 'error'); return; }
    if (pw !== pw2)            { _showResetMsg('Parolele nu coincid.', 'error'); return; }
    const btn = document.getElementById('resetBtn');
    btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Se salvează...';
    document.getElementById('resetMsg').style.display = 'none';
    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/auth/reset-password', {
            method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({token, new_password: pw})
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (res.ok) {
            _showResetMsg('✓ Parolă resetată! Te poți conecta acum.', 'success');
            setTimeout(() => {
                window.history.replaceState({}, '', '/');   // curăță ?reset_token= din URL
                document.getElementById('resetPanel').style.display      = 'none';
                document.getElementById('authMainContent').style.display = 'block';
                switchAuthTab('login');
            }, 2400);
        } else {
            _showResetMsg(data.detail || 'Link invalid sau expirat. Solicită un link nou.', 'error');
        }
    } catch {
        _showResetMsg('Eroare de conexiune. Verifică că serverul rulează.', 'error');
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Setează parola';
    }
}
// Auto-detect ?reset_token= la încărcarea paginii
(function checkResetToken() {
    const token = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('reset_token');
    if (!token) return;
    fetch(`/auth/validate-token?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (data.valid) {
                document.getElementById('authMainContent').style.display = 'none';
                document.getElementById('forgotPanel').style.display     = 'none';
                document.getElementById('resetPanel').style.display      = 'block';
                setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('resetPassword').focus(), 60);
            } else {
                showAuthMsg('Link de resetare invalid sau expirat. Solicită un link nou din pagina de conectare.', 'error');
            }
        })
        .catch(() => {/* server pornit? nu blocăm pagina */});
})();

// Auto-detect ?verify_token= la încărcarea paginii
(function checkVerifyToken() {
    const token = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('verify_token');
    if (!token) return;
    fetch(`/auth/verify-email?token=${encodeURIComponent(token)}`)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(data => {
            window.history.replaceState({}, '', '/'); // curăță ?verify_token= din URL
            showAuthOverlay?.();                      // asigură că overlay-ul e vizibil
            if (data.ok) {
                switchAuthTab('login');
                showAuthMsg('✅ Email confirmat! Te poți conecta acum.', 'success');
            } else {
                showAuthMsg(data.message || 'Link de confirmare invalid.', 'error');
            }
        })
        .catch(() => {});
})();

async function handleAuth(type) {
    const email = document.getElementById('authEmail').value.trim();
    const password = document.getElementById('authPassword').value;
    const btn = document.getElementById('authSubmitBtn');
    if (!email || !password) { showAuthMsg('Completează emailul și parola.', 'error'); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = type==='login' ? 'Se conectează...' : 'Se creează contul...';
    document.getElementById('authMsg').style.display = 'none';
    try {
        let fetchUrl = `/auth/${type}`;
            if (type === 'signup') {
                const pendingRef = localStorage.getItem('_pendingRef') || '';
                if (pendingRef) {
                    fetchUrl += `?ref=${encodeURIComponent(pendingRef)}`;
                }
            }

            const res = await fetch(fetchUrl, { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({email, password}) });
            
            if (res.ok && type === 'signup') {
                localStorage.removeItem('_pendingRef');
            }
        const data = await res.json();
        if (res.ok) {
            if (type==='login') {
                localStorage.setItem('coaching_token', data.access_token);
                const nutRef = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('ref_nut')
    || localStorage.getItem('_pendingNutRef');
if (nutRef) {
    localStorage.removeItem('_pendingNutRef');
    fetch(`/nutritionist/join/${nutRef}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${data.access_token}` }
    }).catch(() => {});
}
                hideAuthOverlay();
                setTimeout(checkOnboarding, 600);
                showSectionNav();
                loadHistory();
                loadProfileAndPrefill();
                loadProgress();
                loadAdaptiveAnalysis();
                loadFoodToday();
                loadExerciseToday();
                loadMealPlanSection();
                loadSettings();
                initPushNotifications();
                loadPremiumStatus();
                _initTargetWeightInput();
                loadTemplates();
            } else {
                showAuthMsg('✓ Cont creat! Te poți conecta acum.', 'success');
                // Schimbăm automat la tab-ul de login după 1.5 secunde
                setTimeout(() => {
                    switchAuthTab('login');
                    document.getElementById('authEmail').value    = email;
                    document.getElementById('authPassword').value = '';
                    document.getElementById('authPassword').focus();
                }, 1500);
            }
        } else { showAuthMsg(data.detail || 'A apărut o eroare. Încearcă din nou.', 'error'); }
    } catch { showAuthMsg('Eroare de conexiune. Verifică că serverul rulează.', 'error'); }
    finally { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = type==='login' ? 'Conectare' : 'Creează Cont'; }
}

// ── SECTION NAVIGATION ───────────────────────────────────────
function switchTab(name) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.stab-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.add('stab-hidden'));
    document.querySelectorAll('.snav-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
    const panelId = 'tab' + name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + name.slice(1);
    const panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
    const tab   = document.querySelector(`.snav-tab[data-tab="${name}"]`);
    if (panel) { panel.classList.remove('stab-hidden'); }
    if (tab)   { tab.classList.add('active'); }
}
function showSectionNav() {
    const nav = document.getElementById('sectionNav');
    if (!nav) return;
    nav.style.display = 'block';
    switchTab('historic');
}

// ── BODY COMP ────────────────────────────────────────────────
enableBodyComp.addEventListener('change', () => {
    bodyCompSection.style.display = enableBodyComp.checked ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (enableBodyComp.checked) updateBodyTypeBfValues();
});
function setMode(mode) {
    const visual = document.getElementById('bcVisualSection');
    const manual = document.getElementById('bcManualSection');
    document.getElementById('btnModeVisual').classList.toggle('active', mode==='visual');
    document.getElementById('btnModeManual').classList.toggle('active', mode==='manual');
    visual.style.display = mode==='visual' ? 'block' : 'none';
    manual.style.display = mode==='manual' ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (mode==='visual') document.getElementById('bfManualInput').value = '';
    else document.querySelectorAll('input[name="body_type"]').forEach(r => r.checked = false);
}
function updateBodyTypeBfValues() {
    const sex = document.querySelector('input[name="sex"]:checked')?.value || 'm';
    document.querySelectorAll('.body-type-option').forEach(opt => {
        const bf = opt.dataset[`bf${sex.toUpperCase()}`];
        const el = opt.querySelector('.bf-val');
        if (el && bf) el.textContent = bf;
    });
}
document.querySelectorAll('input[name="sex"]').forEach(r => r.addEventListener('change', updateBodyTypeBfValues));

// ── LOADING ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
function setLoading(loading) {
    submitBtn.disabled = loading;
    submitBtn.classList.toggle('loading', loading);
    btnText.textContent = loading ? 'Se generează planul...' : 'Calculează planul meu';
}
function setPdfLoading(loading) {
    btnPdf.disabled = loading;
    btnPdf.classList.toggle('loading', loading);
    document.getElementById('pdfBtnText').textContent = loading ? 'Se generează PDF-ul...' : '⬇  Descarcă Raportul PDF';
    document.getElementById('pdfSpinner').style.display = loading ? 'block' : 'none';
}

// ── FORM SUBMIT ──────────────────────────────────────────────
form.addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    errorBox.style.display = 'none';
    insightCard.style.display = 'none';
    insightCard.classList.remove('visible');
    const payload = {
        name: document.getElementById('name').value.trim(),
        weight_kg: parseFloat(document.getElementById('weight').value),
        height_cm: parseFloat(document.getElementById('height').value),
        age: parseInt(document.getElementById('age').value),
        sex: document.querySelector('input[name="sex"]:checked').value,
        activity_level: document.getElementById('activity').value,
        goal: document.querySelector('input[name="goal"]:checked').value,
    };
    if (enableBodyComp.checked) {
        const bfManual = parseFloat(document.getElementById('bfManualInput').value);
        const bodyTypeEl = document.querySelector('input[name="body_type"]:checked');
        if (!isNaN(bfManual) && bfManual>=3 && bfManual<=65) payload.body_fat_pct = bfManual;
        else if (bodyTypeEl) payload.body_type = bodyTypeEl.value;
    }
    try {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
        const calcHeaders = {'Content-Type':'application/json'};
        if (token) calcHeaders['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${token}`;
        const res = await fetch('/calculate', { method:'POST', headers:calcHeaders, body:JSON.stringify(payload) });
        if (!res.ok) { const err = await res.json(); throw new Error(err.detail||'Eroare la calcul.'); }
        const data = await res.json();
        lastPayload = payload; lastCalcData = data;
        displayResults(data);
        if (token) {
            fetch('/profile/save', {
                method:'POST',
                headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`},
                body:JSON.stringify({ height_cm:payload.height_cm, age:payload.age, sex:payload.sex, activity_level:payload.activity_level, goal:payload.goal, initial_weight_kg:payload.weight_kg }),
            }).catch(()=>{});
        }
    } catch (err) {
        errorBox.textContent = `⚠  ${err.message}`; errorBox.style.display = 'block';
    } finally { setLoading(false); }
});

// ── DISPLAY RESULTS ──────────────────────────────────────────
function displayResults(data) {
    const m = data.macros; const total = m.total_kcal;
    document.getElementById('clientName').textContent = data.client;
    document.getElementById('targetBadge').textContent = `${data.target_calories} kcal/zi`;
    document.getElementById('weeklyBadge').textContent = data.weekly_change?.display||'';
    document.getElementById('bmrVal').innerHTML = `${data.bmr}<small> kcal</small>`;
    document.getElementById('tdeeVal').innerHTML = `${data.tdee}<small> kcal</small>`;
    document.getElementById('targetVal').innerHTML = `${data.target_calories}<small> kcal</small>`;
    document.getElementById('proteinG').innerHTML = `${m.protein_g}<small>g</small>`;
    document.getElementById('proteinKcal').textContent = `${Math.round(m.protein_kcal)} kcal`;
    document.getElementById('carbsG').innerHTML = `${m.carbs_g}<small>g</small>`;
    document.getElementById('carbsKcal').textContent = `${Math.round(m.carbs_kcal)} kcal`;
    document.getElementById('fatG').innerHTML = `${m.fat_g}<small>g</small>`;
    document.getElementById('fatKcal').textContent = `${Math.round(m.fat_kcal)} kcal`;
    const pPct = Math.round((m.protein_kcal/total)*100);
    const cPct = Math.round((m.carbs_kcal/total)*100);
    const fPct = 100-pPct-cPct;
    document.getElementById('legProtein').textContent = `Proteină ${pPct}%`;
    document.getElementById('legCarbs').textContent = `Carbohidrați ${cPct}%`;
    document.getElementById('legFat').textContent = `Grăsimi ${fPct}%`;
    const fb = document.getElementById('formulaBadge');
    fb.textContent = data.formula_used||'Mifflin-St Jeor';
    fb.className = 'formula-badge'+(data.formula_used?.includes('Katch')?' katch-active':'');
    const lbmRow = document.getElementById('lbmInfoRow');
    if (data.lean_body_mass_kg && data.estimated_bf_pct) {
        const fm = (parseFloat(document.getElementById('weight').value)-data.lean_body_mass_kg).toFixed(1);
        document.getElementById('lbmVal').textContent = `${data.lean_body_mass_kg} kg`;
        document.getElementById('bfVal').textContent = `${data.estimated_bf_pct}%`;
        document.getElementById('fatMassVal').textContent = `${fm} kg`;
        lbmRow.classList.add('visible');
    } else { lbmRow.classList.remove('visible'); }
    results.classList.add('visible');
    results.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});
    setTimeout(() => {
        document.getElementById('proteinBar').style.width = `${pPct}%`;
        document.getElementById('carbsBar').style.width = `${cPct}%`;
        document.getElementById('fatBar').style.width = `${fPct}%`;
        document.getElementById('distProtein').style.width = `${pPct}%`;
        document.getElementById('distCarbs').style.width = `${cPct}%`;
        document.getElementById('distFat').style.width = `${fPct}%`;
    }, 100);
    if (data.coaching_insight||data.insight) {
        insightText.textContent = (data.coaching_insight||data.insight).replace(/_/g,'');
        insightCard.style.display = 'block';
        setTimeout(()=>insightCard.classList.add('visible'), 250);
    }
    btnPdf.classList.add('visible');
}

// ── PDF ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function downloadReport() {
    if (!lastCalcData||!lastPayload) return;
    setPdfLoading(true);
    const goalDisplayMap = {cut_bland:'Tăiere Blândă −200 kcal/zi',cut:'Tăiere Moderată −400 kcal/zi',mentinere:'Menținere / Recompoziție',bulk_lean:'Lean Bulk +200 kcal/zi',bulk:'Bulk Moderat +400 kcal/zi'};
    const m = lastCalcData.macros;
    const reportPayload = {
        client_name:lastCalcData.client, age:lastPayload.age, sex:lastPayload.sex,
        weight_kg:lastPayload.weight_kg, height_cm:lastPayload.height_cm,
        activity_level:lastPayload.activity_level, goal:lastPayload.goal,
        goal_display:goalDisplayMap[lastPayload.goal]||lastPayload.goal,
        weekly_display:lastCalcData.weekly_change?.display||'',
        bmr:lastCalcData.bmr, tdee:lastCalcData.tdee,
        target_calories:lastCalcData.target_calories,
        formula_used:lastCalcData.formula_used||'Mifflin-St Jeor',
        estimated_bf_pct:lastCalcData.estimated_bf_pct||null,
        lean_body_mass_kg:lastCalcData.lean_body_mass_kg||null,
        protein_g:m.protein_g, protein_kcal:Math.round(m.protein_kcal),
        carbs_g:m.carbs_g, carbs_kcal:Math.round(m.carbs_kcal),
        fat_g:m.fat_g, fat_kcal:Math.round(m.fat_kcal), total_kcal:m.total_kcal,
        coaching_insight:lastCalcData.coaching_insight||'',
    };
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/report', {method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(reportPayload)});
        if (!res.ok) { const err=await res.json(); throw new Error(err.detail||'Eroare PDF.'); }
        const blob = await res.blob();
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = url; link.download = `coaching_${lastCalcData.client}_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.pdf`;
        document.body.appendChild(link); link.click(); document.body.removeChild(link); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch (err) { alert(`Eroare PDF: ${err.message}`); }
    finally { setPdfLoading(false); }
}

// ── HISTORY ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
function getUserEmailFromToken() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return null;
    try { return JSON.parse(atob(token.split('.')[1])).sub||null; } catch { return null; }
}
const GOAL_MAP = {cut_bland:'Tăiere Blândă',cut:'Tăiere Moderată',mentinere:'Menținere',bulk_lean:'Lean Bulk',bulk:'Bulk Moderat'};

async function loadHistory(attempt = 1) {
    const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3;
    const RETRY_DELAY  = 1500; // ms — suficient pentru Neon cold start

    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const content = document.getElementById('historyContent');
    const pill    = document.getElementById('historyUserPill');

    if (attempt === 1) {
        content.innerHTML = '<p class="history-empty">Se încarcă istoricul...</p>';
        const email = getUserEmailFromToken();
        pill.innerHTML = email
            ? `<div class="history-user-pill">Logat ca <strong>${email}</strong></div>`
            : '';
    }

    try {
        const res = await fetch('/auth/my-sessions', {
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
        });
        if (res.status === 401) { handleUnauthorized(); return; }
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Eroare server');

        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.count === 0) {
            content.innerHTML = `<p class="history-empty">Niciun calcul salvat încă.<br>Completează formularul de mai sus pentru a începe!</p>`;
            return;
        }
        renderHistory(data.sessions, data.count);

    } catch {
        if (attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
            // Baza de date Neon.tech e în cold start — retry silențios
            content.innerHTML = `<p class="history-empty" style="color:var(--text-secondary)">Se reconectează... (${attempt}/${MAX_ATTEMPTS})</p>`;
            setTimeout(() => loadHistory(attempt + 1), RETRY_DELAY);
        } else {
            // Toate încercările au eșuat → abia acum arătăm eroarea
            content.innerHTML = `<p class="history-empty" style="color:#ff7070">
                Eroare la încărcarea istoricului. Încearcă din nou.
            </p>`;
        }
    }
}
function renderHistory(sessions, count) {
    const rows = sessions.map((s,i) => {
        const date = s.timestamp ? s.timestamp.replace('T',' ').slice(0,16) : '—';
        const goal = GOAL_MAP[s.goal]||s.goal||'—';
        const hasLBM = s.lbm_kg && s.estimated_bf;
        const insightHtml = s.coaching_insight ? s.coaching_insight.replace(/_/g,'').replace(/\n\n/g,'<br><br>').replace(/\n/g,'<br>') : null;
        return `<div class="history-row" id="hrow-${i}">
            <div class="history-main" onclick="toggleDetails(${i})">
                <div class="history-col"><span class="history-label">Data</span><span class="history-val" style="font-size:12.5px">${date}</span></div>
                <div class="history-col"><span class="history-label">Greutate</span><span class="history-val">${s.weight_kg??'—'}<small> kg</small></span></div>
                <div class="history-col"><span class="history-label">TDEE</span><span class="history-val">${s.tdee??'—'}<small> kcal</small></span></div>
                <div class="history-col"><span class="history-label">Obiectiv</span><span class="history-goal-chip">${goal}</span></div>
                <div class="history-col history-col-action"><span class="history-target-pill">${s.target_kcal??'—'} kcal</span><span class="history-toggle" id="htoggle-${i}">+ detalii</span></div>
            </div>
            <div class="history-details" id="hdetails-${i}" style="display:none">
                <div class="history-macros">
                    <div class="hm-item protein-col"><span class="hm-label">Proteină</span><span class="hm-val">${s.protein_g??'—'}<small style="font-size:11px;font-weight:400;color:var(--text-secondary)">g</small></span></div>
                    <div class="hm-item carbs-col"><span class="hm-label">Carbohidrați</span><span class="hm-val">${s.carbs_g??'—'}<small style="font-size:11px;font-weight:400;color:var(--text-secondary)">g</small></span></div>
                    <div class="hm-item fat-col"><span class="hm-label">Grăsimi</span><span class="hm-val">${s.fat_g??'—'}<small style="font-size:11px;font-weight:400;color:var(--text-secondary)">g</small></span></div>
                    <div class="hm-item"><span class="hm-label">BMR</span><span class="hm-val" style="font-size:13px">${s.bmr??'—'} <small style="font-weight:400;color:var(--text-secondary)">kcal</small></span></div>
                    <div class="hm-item"><span class="hm-label">Formulă</span><span class="hm-val" style="font-size:11px;padding-top:3px">${s.formula_used??'Mifflin'}</span></div>
                    ${hasLBM?`<div class="hm-item"><span class="hm-label">LBM</span><span class="hm-val" style="font-size:13px">${s.lbm_kg} <small style="font-weight:400;color:var(--text-secondary)">kg</small></span></div><div class="hm-item"><span class="hm-label">BF%</span><span class="hm-val" style="font-size:13px">${s.estimated_bf}<small style="font-weight:400;color:var(--text-secondary)">%</small></span></div>`:''}
                </div>
                ${insightHtml?`<div class="history-insight"><span class="history-insight-label">✦ AI Insight</span><p class="history-insight-text">${insightHtml}</p></div>`:''}
            </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');
    document.getElementById('historyContent').innerHTML = `<p class="history-count">${count} calcul${count!==1?'e':''} salvat${count!==1?'e':''}</p><div class="history-list">${rows}</div>`;
}
function toggleDetails(i) {
    const d=document.getElementById(`hdetails-${i}`); const t=document.getElementById(`htoggle-${i}`);
    const o=d.style.display==='none'; d.style.display=o?'block':'none'; t.textContent=o?'− ascunde':'+ detalii';
}

// ── PROGRESS ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function loadProgress(attempt = 1) {
    const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3;
    const RETRY_DELAY  = 1500;
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/checkin/history', {headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`}});
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Eroare server');
        const data = await res.json();
        renderProgress(data.checkins);
    } catch(e) {
        if (attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
            setTimeout(() => loadProgress(attempt + 1), RETRY_DELAY);
        } else {
            console.error('Progress load error după', MAX_ATTEMPTS, 'încercări:', e);
        }
    }
}
function renderProgress(checkins) {
    if (!checkins||checkins.length===0) {
        document.getElementById('progressStats').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('chartCard').style.display     = 'none';
    // Empty state în locul graficului
    const existingEmpty = document.getElementById('progressEmptyState');
    if (!existingEmpty) {
        const el = document.createElement('div');
        el.id = 'progressEmptyState';
        el.innerHTML = `
            <div class="empty-state" style="padding:52px 20px">
                <div class="empty-state-icon">📈</div>
                <div class="empty-state-title">Niciun check-in încă</div>
                <div class="empty-state-sub">
                    Loghează greutatea de azi și revino mâine. Graficul apare
                    după 2 check-in-uri — trendurile se văd în săptămâni.
                </div>
                <button onclick="document.getElementById('checkinWeight').focus()"
                        style="background:var(--accent);color:#fff;border:none;
                               border-radius:var(--radius-sm);padding:11px 22px;
                               font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:13px;
                               font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;margin-top:4px;
                               transition:opacity .15s;"
                        onmouseover="this.style.opacity='.85'"
                        onmouseout="this.style.opacity='1'">
                    + Adaugă primul check-in
                </button>
            </div>`;
        const section = document.getElementById('progressSection');
        if (section) section.appendChild(el);
    } else {
        existingEmpty.style.display = 'block';
    }
    return;
    }
    const first=checkins[0]; const last=checkins[checkins.length-1];
    const change=+(last.weight_kg-first.weight_kg).toFixed(1);
    const prefix=change>0?'+':'';
    document.getElementById('statStart').textContent = `${first.weight_kg} kg`;
    document.getElementById('statCurrent').textContent = `${last.weight_kg} kg`;
    document.getElementById('statChange').textContent = `${prefix}${change} kg`;
    document.getElementById('statChange').style.color = change<0?'var(--green)':change>0?'var(--accent)':'var(--text-primary)';
    document.getElementById('statCount').textContent = checkins.length;
    document.getElementById('progressStats').style.display = 'block';
    const emptyEl = document.getElementById('progressEmptyState');
    if (emptyEl) emptyEl.style.display = 'none';
    if (checkins.length>=2) {
        _lastCheckins = checkins;
        document.getElementById('chartCard').style.display='block';
        renderWeightChart(checkins);
    } else { document.getElementById('chartCard').style.display='none'; }
}
function renderWeightChart(checkins) {
    const n = checkins.length;

    // ── Labels și greutăți existente ────────────────────────────────────────
    const existingLabels = checkins.map(c => {
        const d = new Date(c.date + 'T00:00:00');
        return `${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}.${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}`;
    });
    const weights = checkins.map(c => parseFloat(c.weight_kg));

    // ── Medie mobilă 7 zile ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const movAvg = weights.map((_, i, arr) => {
        const slice = arr.slice(Math.max(0, i - 6), i + 1);
        return Math.round(slice.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / slice.length * 10) / 10;
    });

    // ── Regresie liniară simplă (OLS) ────────────────────────────────────────
    const xMean = (n - 1) / 2;
    const yMean = weights.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
    const num   = weights.reduce((s, y, i) => s + (i - xMean) * (y - yMean), 0);
    const den   = weights.reduce((s, _, i) => s + (i - xMean) ** 2, 0);
    const slope = den ? num / den : 0;   // kg per check-in

    const trendLine = weights.map((_, i) =>
        Math.round((yMean + slope * (i - xMean)) * 10) / 10
    );

    // ── Slope în kg/săptămână ───────────────────────────────────────────────
    const daySpan        = n >= 2
        ? (new Date(checkins[n-1].date) - new Date(checkins[0].date)) / 86400000
        : 7;
    const avgDaysBetween = daySpan / Math.max(n - 1, 1);
    const slopePerWeek   = slope * (7 / Math.max(avgDaysBetween, 0.5));

    // ── Proiecție viitoare — 8 săptămâni ────────────────────────────────────
    const FUTURE_WEEKS  = 8;
    const lastDate      = new Date(checkins[n-1].date + 'T00:00:00');
    const lastWeight    = weights[n - 1];
    const futureLabels  = [];
    const futureWeights = [];

    for (let w = 1; w <= FUTURE_WEEKS; w++) {
        const fd = new Date(lastDate);
        fd.setDate(fd.getDate() + w * 7);
        futureLabels.push(
            `${String(fd.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}.${String(fd.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}`
        );
        futureWeights.push(
            Math.round((lastWeight + slopePerWeek * w) * 10) / 10
        );
    }

    // ── Combinăm date existente + viitoare ──────────────────────────────────
    const allLabels     = [...existingLabels, ...futureLabels];
    const weightsExt    = [...weights,   ...Array(FUTURE_WEEKS).fill(null)];
    const movAvgExt     = [...movAvg,    ...Array(FUTURE_WEEKS).fill(null)];
    const trendLineExt  = [...trendLine, ...Array(FUTURE_WEEKS).fill(null)];

    // Proiecție: null pentru zonă istorică, valori pentru viitor
    // Punctul de joncțiune (index n-1) = lastWeight → linie continuă fără salt
    const projectionData = [...Array(n).fill(null), ...futureWeights];
    projectionData[n - 1] = lastWeight;

    // ── Text predicție actualizat ────────────────────────────────────────────
    const predEl = document.getElementById('weightPrediction');
    if (predEl) {
        const absRate    = Math.abs(slopePerWeek);
        const targetVal  = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('_targetWeight') || '');
        const hasTarget  = !isNaN(targetVal) && targetVal > 20 && targetVal < 300;

        if (absRate < 0.04) {
            predEl.style.display     = 'block';
            predEl.style.borderColor = 'var(--green)';
            predEl.innerHTML = '⚖️ Greutatea ta este stabilă — ideal pentru recompoziție corporală.';
        } else {
            const dir = slopePerWeek < 0 ? '↓ scazi' : '↑ crești';
            const col = slopePerWeek < 0 ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--accent)';
            let etaHtml = '';

            if (hasTarget) {
                const diff  = targetVal - lastWeight;
                const weeks = slopePerWeek !== 0 ? diff / slopePerWeek : null;
                if (weeks !== null && Math.round(weeks) > 0 && weeks < 260) {
                    etaHtml = ` &nbsp;·&nbsp; <strong style="color:var(--text-primary)">La ${targetVal} kg în ~${Math.round(weeks)} săpt.</strong>`;
                } else if (weeks !== null && Math.round(weeks) <= 0) {
                    etaHtml = ` &nbsp;·&nbsp; <strong style="color:var(--green)">Ai atins sau depășit ținta! 🎯</strong>`;
                }
            } else {
                const pred8 = Math.round((lastWeight + slopePerWeek * 8) * 10) / 10;
                etaHtml = ` &nbsp;·&nbsp; 8 săpt.: <strong style="color:var(--text-primary)">${pred8} kg</strong>`;
            }

            predEl.style.display     = 'block';
            predEl.style.borderColor = col;
            predEl.innerHTML =
                `📊 Trend: <strong style="color:${col}">${dir} ${absRate.toFixed(2)} kg/săpt.</strong>` +
                etaHtml;
        }
    }

    // ── Randare Chart.js ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const ctx = document.getElementById('weightChart').getContext('2d');
    if (weightChartInstance) { weightChartInstance.destroy(); weightChartInstance = null; }

    weightChartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: 'line',
        data: {
            labels: allLabels,
            datasets: [
                // Dataset 1: greutatea reală
                {
                    label:                     'Greutate',
                    data:                      weightsExt,
                    borderColor:               '#c4622d',
                    backgroundColor:           'rgba(196,98,45,0.07)',
                    pointBackgroundColor:       '#c4622d',
                    pointBorderColor:           '#080808',
                    pointBorderWidth:           2,
                    pointRadius:               5,
                    pointHoverRadius:          7,
                    pointHoverBackgroundColor: '#d4722d',
                    tension:                   0.35,
                    fill:                      true,
                    borderWidth:               2,
                    order:                     1,
                    spanGaps:                  false,
                },
                // Dataset 2: medie mobilă 7 zile
                {
                    label:            'Medie 7 zile',
                    data:             movAvgExt,
                    borderColor:      'rgba(91,156,246,0.7)',
                    backgroundColor:  'transparent',
                    pointRadius:      0,
                    pointHoverRadius: 0,
                    borderWidth:      1.5,
                    borderDash:       [5, 3],
                    tension:          0.4,
                    fill:             false,
                    order:            2,
                    spanGaps:         false,
                },
                // Dataset 3: trend OLS (zona istorică)
                {
                    label:            'Trend',
                    data:             trendLineExt,
                    borderColor:      'rgba(74,222,128,0.35)',
                    backgroundColor:  'transparent',
                    pointRadius:      0,
                    pointHoverRadius: 0,
                    borderWidth:      1,
                    borderDash:       [2, 5],
                    tension:          0,
                    fill:             false,
                    order:            3,
                    spanGaps:         false,
                },
                // Dataset 4: proiecție viitoare (portocaliu deschis, punctat)
                {
                    label:            'Proiecție',
                    data:             projectionData,
                    borderColor:      'rgba(196,98,45,0.4)',
                    backgroundColor:  'transparent',
                    pointRadius:      function(ctx) { return ctx.dataIndex >= n ? 3 : 0; },
                    pointHoverRadius: function(ctx) { return ctx.dataIndex >= n ? 5 : 0; },
                    pointBackgroundColor: 'rgba(196,98,45,0.55)',
                    borderWidth:      1.5,
                    borderDash:       [4, 4],
                    tension:          0,
                    fill:             false,
                    order:            4,
                    spanGaps:         false,
                },
            ],
        },
        options: {
            responsive:          true,
            maintainAspectRatio: false,
            interaction:         { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
            plugins: {
                legend: {
                    display:  true,
                    position: 'bottom',
                    labels: {
                        color:         '#6b6560',
                        font:          { family: 'Inter', size: 10 },
                        boxWidth:      18,
                        padding:       12,
                        usePointStyle: true,
                        filter: item => item.text !== 'Trend',   // trendul OLS ascuns din legendă
                    },
                },
                tooltip: {
                    backgroundColor: '#161616',
                    borderColor:     '#2e2e2e',
                    borderWidth:     1,
                    titleColor:      '#8e8880',
                    bodyColor:       '#c4622d',
                    padding:         12,
                    titleFont:       { family: 'Inter', size: 11, weight: '500' },
                    bodyFont:        { family: 'Inter', size: 15, weight: '700' },
                    callbacks: {
                        title: items => items[0].label,
                        label: ctx => {
                            if (ctx.datasetIndex === 0 && ctx.parsed.y !== null)
                                return `  Greutate: ${ctx.parsed.y} kg`;
                            if (ctx.datasetIndex === 1 && ctx.parsed.y !== null)
                                return `  Medie 7 zile: ${ctx.parsed.y} kg`;
                            if (ctx.datasetIndex === 3 && ctx.parsed.y !== null && ctx.dataIndex >= n)
                                return `  Proiecție: ${ctx.parsed.y} kg`;
                            return null;
                        },
                    },
                },
            },
            scales: {
                x: {
                    grid:   { color: '#1a1a1a' },
                    ticks:  {
                        color: function(ctx) {
                            return ctx.index >= n ? 'rgba(196,98,45,0.45)' : '#6b6560';
                        },
                        font: { family: 'Inter', size: 11 },
                    },
                    border: { display: false },
                },
                y: {
                    grid:   { color: '#1a1a1a' },
                    ticks:  { color: '#6b6560', font: { family: 'Inter', size: 11 }, callback: v => `${v} kg` },
                    border: { display: false },
                },
            },
        },
    });
}

// ── COACH RECOMMENDATIONS (client vede mesajele coachului) ────────────────
async function loadCoachRecommendations() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const [unreadRes, recsRes] = await Promise.all([
            fetch('/coach/unread-count',       { headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` } }),
            fetch('/coach/my-recommendations', { headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` } }),
        ]);
        if (!unreadRes.ok || !recsRes.ok) return;

        const { unread }            = await unreadRes.json();
        const { recommendations: recs } = await recsRes.json();

        if (!recs || recs.length === 0) return;

        // Badge toast dacă există necitite
        if (unread > 0) {
            toast(`${unread} recomandare nouă de la coach ✦`, 'info', 6000);
        }

        // Afișează cardul în Settings
        const card = document.getElementById('coachRecsCard');
        if (card) card.style.display = 'block';
        renderCoachRecsInSettings(recs.slice(0, 5));
    } catch(e) {}
}

function renderCoachRecsInSettings(recs) {
    const container = document.getElementById('coachRecsInSettings');
    if (!container || !recs.length) return;
    container.innerHTML = recs.map(r => `
        <div style="background:var(--surface-2);border:1px solid var(--border);
                    border-radius:var(--radius-sm);padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:8px">
            <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text-primary);line-height:1.6;margin:0 0 6px">
                ${escapeHtml(r.message)}</p>
            <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-muted);margin:0">
                ${(r.created_at||'').slice(0,16).replace('T',' ')}</p>
        </div>`).join('');
}

// ── REFERRAL SYSTEM ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function initReferralCard() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/referral/me', { headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` } });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();

        document.getElementById('refLoadingState').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('refContent').style.display      = 'block';
        document.getElementById('refCodeDisplay').textContent    = data.code;
        document.getElementById('refLinkHidden').value           = data.link;

        countUp(document.getElementById('refInvited'),   data.stats.invited,       400);
        countUp(document.getElementById('refCompleted'), data.stats.completed,     400);
        countUp(document.getElementById('refMonths'),    data.stats.months_earned, 400);
    } catch(e) {
        document.getElementById('refLoadingState').textContent = '';
    }
}

async function copyRefLink() {
    const link = document.getElementById('refLinkHidden')?.value;
    if (!link) return;
    try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(link);
        toast('Link de invitație copiat! ✓', 'success');
    } catch(e) {
        toast('Copiază manual: ' + link, 'info', 6000);
    }
}

// Citește ?ref= din URL la landing și îl stochează până la signup
(function() {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const ref    = params.get('ref');
    if (ref) {
        localStorage.setItem('_pendingRef', ref);
        // Curăță URL-ul fără reload
        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
        url.searchParams.delete('ref');
        window.history.replaceState({}, '', url.toString());
    }
})();

(function() {
    const p = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('ref_nut');
    if (p) {
        localStorage.setItem('_pendingNutRef', p);
        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
        url.searchParams.delete('ref_nut');
        window.history.replaceState({}, '', url.toString());
    }
})();

// ── SHARE LINK ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function initShareCard() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/share/me', { headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` } });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.share_url) _showShareLink(data.share_url);
    } catch(e) {}
}

async function generateShareLink() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/share/generate', { method:'POST', headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` } });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        _showShareLink(data.share_url);
        toast('Link partajabil generat! ✓', 'success');
    } catch(e) {}
}

async function revokeShareLink() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        await fetch('/share/revoke', { method:'DELETE', headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` } });
        document.getElementById('shareLinkWrap').style.display    = 'none';
        document.getElementById('shareGenerateWrap').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('shareLinkInput').value            = '';
        toast('Link revocat.', 'info');
    } catch(e) {}
}

async function copyShareLink() {
    const url = document.getElementById('shareLinkInput').value;
    if (!url) return;
    try {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
        toast('Link copiat în clipboard! ✓', 'success');
    } catch(e) {
        document.getElementById('shareLinkInput').select();
        toast('Selectat — Ctrl+C pentru copiat', 'info');
    }
}

function _showShareLink(url) {
    document.getElementById('shareLinkInput').value              = url;
    document.getElementById('shareLinkWrap').style.display       = 'flex';
    document.getElementById('shareLinkWrap').style.flexDirection = 'column';
    document.getElementById('shareGenerateWrap').style.display   = 'none';
}

function _shareMsg(msg) {
    const el = document.getElementById('shareFeedback');
    el.textContent   = msg;
    el.style.display = 'block';
    setTimeout(() => { el.style.display = 'none'; }, 3000);
}

// ── MICRONUTRIENT SPOTLIGHT ───────────────────────────────────────────────
const RISK_COLORS = { ridicat:'#ef4444', moderat:'#f59e0b', scăzut:'rgba(74,222,128,0.8)' };
const RISK_BG     = { ridicat:'rgba(239,68,68,0.07)', moderat:'rgba(245,158,11,0.07)', scăzut:'rgba(74,222,128,0.05)' };
const RISK_BORDER = { ridicat:'rgba(239,68,68,0.25)', moderat:'rgba(245,158,11,0.25)', scăzut:'rgba(74,222,128,0.2)' };

async function loadMicronutrientSpotlight(force = false) {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const content = document.getElementById('microContent');
    if (!content) return;

    // Skeleton loading
    content.innerHTML = `
        <div class="skeleton-card"><div class="skeleton skeleton-line lg"></div><div class="skeleton skeleton-line sm"></div></div>
        <div class="skeleton-card"><div class="skeleton skeleton-line lg"></div><div class="skeleton skeleton-line sm"></div></div>
        <div class="skeleton-card"><div class="skeleton skeleton-line lg"></div><div class="skeleton skeleton-line sm"></div></div>`;

    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/analytics/micronutrient-spotlight', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
        });

        if (res.status === 402) {
            content.innerHTML = `
                <div style="text-align:center;padding:20px 0;">
                    <p style="font-size:14px;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-bottom:16px">
                        Micronutrient Spotlight este disponibil cu <strong style="color:var(--accent)">Premium ✦</strong></p>
                    <button onclick="startStripeCheckout()" style="background:var(--accent);border:none;border-radius:var(--radius-sm);
                            color:#fff;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;padding:10px 22px;font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;">
                        Activează Premium</button>
                </div>`;
            return;
        }

        const data = await res.json();

        if (data.empty || data.error || !data.nutrients?.length) {
            content.innerHTML = `<p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:10px 0">${data.disclaimer || 'Insuficiente date pentru analiză.'}</p>`;
            return;
        }

        // Cache indicator
        const cacheNote = data.cached
            ? `<p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-muted);margin-bottom:14px;">Analiză din această săptămână · ${data.week_of}</p>`
            : `<p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-muted);margin-bottom:14px;">Analiză nouă · ${data.week_of}</p>`;

        const cards = data.nutrients.map(n => {
            const col = RISK_COLORS[n.risk] || RISK_COLORS['moderat'];
            const bg  = RISK_BG[n.risk]     || RISK_BG['moderat'];
            const bdr = RISK_BORDER[n.risk]  || RISK_BORDER['moderat'];
            const foods = (n.foods || []).map(f =>
                `<span style="font-size:11px;background:var(--surface-2);border:1px solid var(--border);
                              border-radius:999px;padding:2px 9px;color:var(--text-secondary)">${f}</span>`
            ).join('');
            return `
                <div style="background:${bg};border:1px solid ${bdr};border-radius:var(--radius-sm);
                            padding:14px 16px;margin-bottom:10px;">
                    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px;">
                        <span style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text-primary)">${n.name}</span>
                        <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.08em;text-transform:uppercase;
                                     color:${col};background:${bg};border:1px solid ${bdr};
                                     border-radius:999px;padding:2px 10px;">risc ${n.risk}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text-secondary);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:10px">${n.reasoning}</p>
                    ${foods ? `<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-top:6px">${foods}</div>` : ''}
                </div>`;
        }).join('');

        content.innerHTML = cacheNote + cards;
        document.getElementById('microDisclaimer').style.display = 'block';

        if (!data.cached) toast('Analiză micronutrienți completă ✓', 'success');

    } catch(e) {
        content.innerHTML = `<p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text-secondary);padding:10px 0">Eroare la analiză. Încearcă din nou.</p>`;
        toast('Eroare la Micronutrient Spotlight', 'error');
    }
}

// ── HEATMAP CALENDAR ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function loadHeatmap() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/analytics/heatmap', {
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
        });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();

        document.getElementById('heatmapLoading').style.display  = 'none';
        document.getElementById('heatmapContent').style.display  = 'block';
        document.getElementById('heatmapCard').style.display     = 'block';
        renderHeatmap(data.days, data.target_kcal);
    } catch(e) {
        console.warn('loadHeatmap:', e);
    }
}

function renderHeatmap(days, targetKcal) {
    const inner = document.getElementById('heatmapInner');
    if (!inner || !days || days.length === 0) return;

    // ── Aliniere la Luni ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const firstDow = (new Date(days[0].date + 'T00:00:00').getDay() + 6) % 7;
    const padded   = [...Array(firstDow).fill(null), ...days];
    const numWeeks = Math.ceil(padded.length / 7);
    const weeks    = Array.from({ length: numWeeks }, (_, w) =>
        padded.slice(w * 7, w * 7 + 7)
    );

    // ── Labels zilele săptămânii ───────────────────────────────────────────
    const DAY_LABELS = ['L','','M','','V','','D'];
    let html = '<div class="heatmap-day-labels">';
    for (const lbl of DAY_LABELS) html += `<div class="heatmap-day-label">${lbl}</div>`;
    html += '</div><div class="heatmap-weeks">';

    // ── Coloane săptămânale ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const MONTHS = ['Ian','Feb','Mar','Apr','Mai','Iun','Iul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
    for (const week of weeks) {
        let monthLabel = '';
        for (const cell of week) {
            if (cell) {
                const d = new Date(cell.date + 'T00:00:00');
                if (d.getDate() <= 7) monthLabel = MONTHS[d.getMonth()];
                break;
            }
        }
        html += `<div class="heatmap-week-col"><div class="heatmap-month-hdr">${monthLabel}</div>`;
        for (let d = 0; d < 7; d++) {
            const cell = week[d];
            if (!cell) { html += '<div class="heatmap-cell" style="background:transparent"></div>'; continue; }
            const bg      = _hmColor(cell, targetKcal);
            const tooltip = _hmTooltip(cell, targetKcal);
            html += `<div class="heatmap-cell" style="background:${bg}" title="${tooltip}"></div>`;
        }
        html += '</div>';
    }
    html += '</div>';
    inner.innerHTML = html;

    // ── Legendă ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const legendEl = document.getElementById('heatmapLegend');
    if (legendEl) {
        const noDataCell = `<div class="heatmap-legend-cell" style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.06)"></div>`;
        if (targetKcal) {
            legendEl.innerHTML =
                `${noDataCell} Fără loguri &nbsp;·&nbsp;`+
                `<div class="heatmap-legend-cell" style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.55)"></div> Sub 50%&nbsp;·&nbsp;`+
                `<div class="heatmap-legend-cell" style="background:rgba(251,146,60,0.60)"></div> 50–75%&nbsp;·&nbsp;`+
                `<div class="heatmap-legend-cell" style="background:rgba(234,179,8,0.65)"></div> 75–95%&nbsp;·&nbsp;`+
                `<div class="heatmap-legend-cell" style="background:rgba(34,197,94,0.70)"></div> Ideal`;
        } else {
            legendEl.innerHTML =
                `${noDataCell} Fără loguri &nbsp;·&nbsp;`+
                `<div class="heatmap-legend-cell" style="background:rgba(34,197,94,0.65)"></div> Logat`;
        }
    }
}

function _hmColor(cell, targetKcal) {
    if (!cell.has_data || cell.is_future) return 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
    const cal = cell.calories || 0;
    if (!targetKcal) return cal > 0 ? 'rgba(34,197,94,0.65)' : 'rgba(239,68,68,0.30)';
    const pct = cal / targetKcal;
    if (cal === 0)   return 'rgba(239,68,68,0.35)';
    if (pct < 0.50)  return 'rgba(239,68,68,0.55)';
    if (pct < 0.75)  return 'rgba(251,146,60,0.60)';
    if (pct < 0.95)  return 'rgba(234,179,8,0.65)';
    if (pct <= 1.15) return 'rgba(34,197,94,0.70)';
    return                  'rgba(251,146,60,0.55)';
}

function _hmTooltip(cell, targetKcal) {
    if (!cell.has_data) return `${cell.date}: fără loguri`;
    const cal = cell.calories || 0;
    if (!targetKcal)    return `${cell.date}: ${cal} kcal`;
    const pct = Math.round(cal / targetKcal * 100);
    return `${cell.date}: ${cal} kcal (${pct}% din target)`;
}

function onTargetWeightChange(val) {
    // Salvează în localStorage și re-randează graficul cu ETA actualizat
    const v = parseFloat(val);
    if (!isNaN(v) && v > 20 && v < 300) {
        localStorage.setItem('_targetWeight', v);
    } else {
        localStorage.removeItem('_targetWeight');
    }
    if (_lastCheckins && _lastCheckins.length >= 2) renderWeightChart(_lastCheckins);
}

function _initTargetWeightInput() {
    // Restaurează valoarea din localStorage la încărcarea paginii
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('_targetWeight');
    const el    = document.getElementById('targetWeightInput');
    if (saved && el) el.value = saved;
}
async function saveCheckin() {
    const token=localStorage.getItem('coaching_token'); if(!token) return;
    const weightEl=document.getElementById('checkinWeight'); const weight=parseFloat(weightEl.value);
    const feedback=document.getElementById('checkinFeedback');
    if (isNaN(weight)||weight<20||weight>300) { feedback.textContent='⚠  Introdu o greutate validă între 20 și 300 kg.'; feedback.style.color='#ff7070'; return; }
    const btn=document.getElementById('btnCheckin'); const btnTxt=document.getElementById('checkinBtnText');
    btn.disabled=true; btnTxt.textContent='Se salvează...';
    try {
        const res=await fetch('/checkin/save',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`},body:JSON.stringify({weight_kg:weight,notes:''})});
        if (res.ok) {
            const data=await res.json(); const action=data.updated?'actualizat':'salvat';
            feedback.textContent=`✓ Check-in ${action}: ${weight} kg · astăzi`; feedback.style.color='var(--green)';
            weightEl.value='';
            await loadProgress();
            // Achievement check
        const checkinN = _achBump('nl_checkin_count');
        _checkAchievements('checkin', checkinN);
            await loadAdaptiveAnalysis();
        } else { feedback.textContent='⚠  Eroare la salvare. Încearcă din nou.'; feedback.style.color='#ff7070'; }
    } catch { feedback.textContent='⚠  Eroare de conexiune.'; feedback.style.color='#ff7070'; }
    finally { btn.disabled=false; btnTxt.textContent='Loghează'; }
}

// ── PROFILE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function loadProfileAndPrefill() {
    const token=localStorage.getItem('coaching_token'); if(!token) return;
    try {
        const res=await fetch('/profile/get',{headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`}});
        if(!res.ok) return;
        const {profile:p}=await res.json(); if(!p) return;
        if(p.height_cm) document.getElementById('height').value=p.height_cm;
        if(p.age) document.getElementById('age').value=p.age;
        if(p.sex) { const sb=document.querySelector(`input[name="sex"][value="${p.sex}"]`); if(sb){sb.checked=true;updateBodyTypeBfValues();} }
        if(p.activity_level) document.getElementById('activity').value=p.activity_level;
        if(p.goal) { const gb=document.querySelector(`input[name="goal"][value="${p.goal}"]`); if(gb) gb.checked=true; }
        if(p.initial_weight_kg) document.getElementById('weight').value=p.initial_weight_kg;
        showToast('✓ Profilul tău a fost încărcat automat');
    } catch(e) { console.error('Profile prefill error:',e); }
}
function showToast(message, isError=false) {
    toast(message, isError ? 'error' : 'success');
}

// ── ADAPTIVE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function loadAdaptiveAnalysis() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const body = document.getElementById('adaptiveBody');
    const conf = document.getElementById('adaptiveConfidence');

    // Stare de încărcare
    if (body) body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="adaptive-teaser">
            <div style="width:22px;height:22px;border:2px solid var(--border);
                 border-top-color:var(--accent);border-radius:50%;
                 animation:spin .7s linear infinite;
                 display:inline-block;margin-bottom:14px;"></div>
            <div class="adaptive-teaser-title" style="color:var(--text-secondary);font-size:13px">
                Se analizează datele tale...
            </div>
        </div>`;
    if (conf) conf.textContent = '';

    try {
        const res = await fetch('/adaptive/analysis', { headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` } });

        // Premium required
        if (res.status === 402) {
            if (body) body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="adaptive-teaser">
                    <div class="adaptive-teaser-icon">🔒</div>
                    <div class="adaptive-teaser-title">Motor Adaptiv · Premium</div>
                    <div class="adaptive-teaser-sub">
                        Analizează TDEE-ul tău real din datele de greutate și ajustează
                        automat caloriile când detectează stagnare sau deviere.
                        <br><br>
                        <button onclick="startStripeCheckout()"
                                style="background:var(--accent);color:#fff;border:none;
                                       border-radius:8px;padding:10px 24px;font-size:13px;
                                       font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;letter-spacing:0.02em;
                                       margin-top:4px;transition:opacity .15s;"
                                onmouseover="this.style.opacity='.85'"
                                onmouseout="this.style.opacity='1'">
                            ✦ Activează Premium
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            return;
        }

        // Eroare generală (altele decât 401 care e prins de interceptor)
        if (!res.ok) {
            if (body) body.innerHTML = `
                <div class="adaptive-teaser">
                    <div class="adaptive-teaser-icon">⚠️</div>
                    <div class="adaptive-teaser-title" style="color:#ff8080">Eroare la încărcare</div>
                    <div class="adaptive-teaser-sub">
                        Nu s-a putut obține analiza adaptivă.<br>
                        Reîncarcă pagina sau încearcă din nou mai târziu.
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            return;
        }

        const data = await res.json();
        renderAdaptiveCard(data);

    } catch (e) {
        console.error('Adaptive error:', e);
        if (body) body.innerHTML = `
            <div class="adaptive-teaser">
                <div class="adaptive-teaser-icon">⚠️</div>
                <div class="adaptive-teaser-title" style="color:#ff8080">Eroare de conexiune</div>
                <div class="adaptive-teaser-sub">
                    Verifică conexiunea la internet și încearcă din nou.
                </div>
            </div>`;
    }
}
function renderAdaptiveCard(data) {
    const conf=document.getElementById('adaptiveConfidence');
    const body=document.getElementById('adaptiveBody');
    if (!data.has_enough_data) {
        const have=data.checkins_count||0; const need=data.min_checkins_needed||2;
        const daysNeed=data.min_days_needed||7;
        const dots=Array.from({length:need},(_,i)=>`<div class="adaptive-teaser-dot ${i<have?'done':''}"></div>`).join('');
        conf.textContent='';
        body.innerHTML=`<div class="adaptive-teaser"><div class="adaptive-teaser-icon">🧠</div><div class="adaptive-teaser-title">Motorul se calibrează</div><div class="adaptive-teaser-sub">${data.insufficient_reason}<br>Loghează greutatea zilnic — după ${daysNeed} zile de date,<br>calculez TDEE-ul tău <strong style="color:var(--text-primary)">real</strong> și ajustez planul automat.</div><div class="adaptive-teaser-progress">${dots}</div></div>`;
        return;
    }
    const confMap={high:'Precizie ridicată',medium:'Precizie medie',low:'Estimare preliminară'};
    const confColor={high:'var(--green)',medium:'var(--accent)',low:'var(--text-secondary)'};
    const tdeeConf=data.real_tdee?.confidence||'low';
    conf.textContent=confMap[tdeeConf]||''; conf.style.color=confColor[tdeeConf]||'var(--text-secondary)';
    const rt=data.real_tdee; const diff=rt?.difference??0; const diffSign=diff>0?'+':'';
    const diffClass=diff>50?'green':diff<-50?'red':'accent';
    const diffBox=Math.abs(diff)>50?`<div class="adaptive-tdee-box ${diff>0?'green-box':'accent-box'}"><span class="adaptive-label">Diferență</span><span class="adaptive-big ${diffClass}">${diffSign}${diff}</span><span class="adaptive-unit">kcal/zi față de formulă</span></div>`:'';
    const tr=data.trend; const rate=tr?.rate_kg_per_week??0; const tRate=tr?.target_rate??0;
    const onTrack=tr?.on_track??true; const pct=Math.min(100,Math.max(0,tr?.progress_pct??0));
    const fillColor=onTrack?'var(--green)':'var(--accent)'; const rateClass=onTrack?'good':'warn';
    const rateSign=rate>0?'+':''; const tSign=tRate>0?'+':'';
    const stag=data.stagnation;
    const stagHTML=stag?.detected?`<div class="adaptive-stagnation">⚠ Stagnare detectată · ${stag.days_span} zile fără progres semnificativ (variație ${stag.variance_kg} kg)</div>`:'';
    const rec=data.recommendation;
    const recCls=rec?.urgency==='high'?'urgent':rec?.action==='maintain'?'maintain':'adjust';
    const recIcon=rec?.action==='maintain'?'✓':rec?.action==='increase'?'↑':'↓';
    const recTitle=rec?.action==='maintain'?'Pe track · Menții planul':rec?.action==='increase'?`Ajustare +${rec.calorie_adjustment} kcal/zi`:`Ajustare ${rec.calorie_adjustment} kcal/zi`;
    body.innerHTML=`
        <div class="adaptive-tdee-row">
            <div class="adaptive-tdee-box accent-box"><span class="adaptive-label">TDEE Real (calculat)</span><span class="adaptive-big accent">${rt?.estimated??'—'}</span><span class="adaptive-unit">kcal/zi · ${data.days_tracked} zile de date</span></div>
            <div class="adaptive-tdee-vs">vs</div>
            <div class="adaptive-tdee-box"><span class="adaptive-label">TDEE Formulă</span><span class="adaptive-big">${rt?.formula_tdee??'—'}</span><span class="adaptive-unit">kcal/zi · Mifflin / Katch</span></div>
            ${diffBox}
        </div>
        <span class="adaptive-section-label">Ritm de progres</span>
        <div class="adaptive-trend-header">
            <div class="adaptive-trend-item"><span class="adaptive-label">Real</span><span class="adaptive-trend-val ${rateClass}">${rateSign}${rate} kg/săpt.</span></div>
            <div class="adaptive-trend-item"><span class="adaptive-label">Target</span><span class="adaptive-trend-val">${tSign}${tRate} kg/săpt.</span></div>
            <div class="adaptive-trend-item"><span class="adaptive-label">Săptămâni de date</span><span class="adaptive-trend-val">${tr?.weeks_tracked??'—'}</span></div>
        </div>
        <div class="adaptive-track"><div class="adaptive-fill" id="adaptiveFill" style="background:${fillColor}"></div></div>
        <div class="adaptive-track-labels"><span>0%</span><span>${onTrack?'✓ Pe track':'⚠ Deviere '+Math.abs(tr?.deviation_pct??0)+'%'}</span><span>100%</span></div>
        ${stagHTML}
        <div class="adaptive-rec ${recCls}"><span class="adaptive-rec-icon">${recIcon}</span><div class="adaptive-rec-content"><div class="adaptive-rec-title">${recTitle}</div><div class="adaptive-rec-text">${rec?.reason??'—'}</div></div></div>`;
    setTimeout(()=>{ const f=document.getElementById('adaptiveFill'); if(f) f.style.width=pct+'%'; },150);
}

// ── P4 FOOD LOGGER ───────────────────────────────────────────
let selectedMealType='mic_dejun';
const FOOD_MEAL_LABELS={mic_dejun:'Mic Dejun',gustare:'Gustare',pranz:'Prânz',cina:'Cină',general:'General'};
const FOOD_CONFIDENCE={high:{text:'precizie ridicată',color:'var(--green)'},medium:{text:'precizie medie',color:'var(--accent)'},low:{text:'precizie scăzută',color:'var(--text-secondary)'}};
function selectMealType(type) {
    selectedMealType=type;
    document.querySelectorAll('.meal-tab').forEach(btn=>btn.classList.toggle('active',btn.dataset.type===type));
}
async function logFood() {
    const token=localStorage.getItem('coaching_token'); if(!token) return;
    const input=document.getElementById('foodInput'); const description=input.value.trim();
    if(!description){showFoodError('Scrie ce ai mâncat — orice descriere e ok.');return;}
