    const btn=document.getElementById('btnFoodLog'); const btnTxt=document.getElementById('foodLogBtnText'); const spin=document.getElementById('foodSpinner');
    btn.disabled=true; btnTxt.textContent='Se analizează...'; spin.style.display='block'; hideFoodError();
    try {
        const res=await fetch('/food/log',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`},body:JSON.stringify({description,meal_type:selectedMealType})});
        const data=await res.json();
        if(!res.ok){
            const errMsg = typeof data.detail === 'string'
                ? data.detail
                : (data.detail?.message || 'Cont fără acces premium. Activează abonamentul.');
            showFoodError(errMsg);
            return;
        }
        input.value=''; input.style.height='auto';
        showToast(`✓ Logat: ${data.parsed?.totals?.calories||0} kcal · ${FOOD_MEAL_LABELS[selectedMealType]||'General'}`);
        await loadFoodToday();
        // Achievement check
        const foodN = _achBump('nl_food_count');
        _checkAchievements('food', foodN);
        // #18 — Sugestie template dacă masa e repetitivă (3+ loguri în 60 zile)
        if (data.suggest_template) showTemplateSuggest(data.suggest_template);
    } catch{showFoodError('Eroare de conexiune. Verifică că serverul rulează.');}
    finally{btn.disabled=false;btnTxt.textContent='Analizează';spin.style.display='none';}
}
async function loadFoodToday(attempt = 1) {
    const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3;
    const RETRY_DELAY  = 1500;
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
    // Skeleton în timp ce așteptăm
        const listEl = document.getElementById('foodLogsList');
        if (listEl && !listEl.children.length) listEl.innerHTML = skeletonFoodList(3);
        const res = await fetch('/food/today', {headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`}});
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Eroare server');
        const data = await res.json();
        renderFoodSection(data);
    } catch(e) {
        if (attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
            setTimeout(() => loadFoodToday(attempt + 1), RETRY_DELAY);
        } else {
            console.error('Food load error după', MAX_ATTEMPTS, 'încercări:', e);
        }
    }
}
function renderFoodSection({logs,summary}) {
    const hasLogs=summary.logs_count>0;
    document.getElementById('foodProgressCard').style.display=hasLogs?'block':'none';
    if(hasLogs){renderFoodProgress(summary);renderFoodLogs(logs);}
    else {
        document.getElementById('foodLogsList').innerHTML = `
            <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:16px;padding:22px 4px;opacity:0.55;">
                <div style="flex:1;height:1px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.06)"></div>
                <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text-secondary);letter-spacing:0.09em;text-transform:uppercase;white-space:nowrap;">Nicio masă logată azi</span>
                <div style="flex:1;height:1px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.06)"></div>
            </div>`;
    }
}
function renderFoodProgress(summary) {
    const dt=summary.daily_totals; const pr=summary.progress; const tg=summary.targets;
    document.getElementById('foodCalConsumed').textContent=dt.calories;
    document.getElementById('foodCalTarget').textContent=tg?tg.calories:'—';
    const pillEl=document.getElementById('foodCalRemainingPill');
    if(pr){
        const rem=pr.remaining_kcal;
        pillEl.style.display='inline-block';
        pillEl.textContent=rem>0?`${rem} kcal rămase`:`${Math.abs(rem)} kcal peste`;
        pillEl.classList.toggle('over',rem<=0);
        setTimeout(()=>{
            setFmpBar('fpCal','fpCalPct','fpCalGrams',pr.calories_pct,`${dt.calories}${tg?' / '+tg.calories+' kcal':' kcal'}`);
            setFmpBar('fpProt','fpProtPct','fpProtGrams',pr.protein_pct,`${dt.protein_g}g${tg?' / '+tg.protein_g+'g':''}`);
            setFmpBar('fpCarb','fpCarbPct','fpCarbGrams',pr.carbs_pct,`${dt.carbs_g}g${tg?' / '+tg.carbs_g+'g':''}`);
            setFmpBar('fpFat','fpFatPct','fpFatGrams',pr.fat_pct,`${dt.fat_g}g${tg?' / '+tg.fat_g+'g':''}`);
        },80);
    } else {pillEl.style.display='none';}
}
function setFmpBar(barId,pctId,gramsId,pct,gramsText) {
    const bar=document.getElementById(barId); const pctEl=document.getElementById(pctId); const grmsEl=document.getElementById(gramsId);
    if(bar){bar.style.width=`${Math.min(pct,100)}%`;bar.classList.toggle('over',pct>100);}
    if(pctEl) pctEl.textContent=`${Math.min(pct,100)}%`;
    if(grmsEl) grmsEl.textContent=gramsText||'';
}
function renderFoodLogs(logs) {
    const container=document.getElementById('foodLogsList');
    if(!logs||logs.length===0){container.innerHTML='';return;}
    container.innerHTML=`<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:16px"><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:18px"><p class="card-label" style="margin-bottom:0">Loguri Azi</p><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text-secondary)">${logs.length} intrare${logs.length!==1?'i':''}</span></div>${logs.map(log=>renderFoodLogItem(log)).join('')}</div>`;
}
function renderFoodLogItem(log) {
    const meal=FOOD_MEAL_LABELS[log.meal_type]||'📋 General';
    const conf=FOOD_CONFIDENCE[log.confidence]||FOOD_CONFIDENCE.medium;
    const time=log.created_at?log.created_at.slice(11,16):'';
    const hasItems=Array.isArray(log.items)&&log.items.length>0;
    const bdHtml=hasItems?log.items.map(item=>`<div class="food-breakdown-item"><span class="food-breakdown-name">${escapeHtml(item.name||'—')}</span><span class="food-breakdown-qty">${escapeHtml(item.quantity||'')}</span><span class="food-breakdown-kcal">${item.calories} kcal</span></div>`).join(''):'';
    return `<div class="food-log-item" id="flog-${log.id}">
        <div class="food-log-top"><div class="food-log-meta"><span class="food-log-meal-badge">${meal}</span><span class="food-log-time">${time}</span></div><div class="food-log-actions"><span class="food-log-kcal">${log.calories}<small>kcal</small></span><button class="btn-food-delete" onclick="deleteFood(${log.id})" title="Șterge">×</button></div></div>
        <p class="food-log-desc">${escapeHtml(log.description)}</p>
        <div class="food-log-macros"><span class="food-macro-pill food-prot">P: ${log.protein_g}g</span><span class="food-macro-pill food-carb">C: ${log.carbs_g}g</span><span class="food-macro-pill food-fat">G: ${log.fat_g}g</span><span class="food-confidence" style="color:${conf.color}">· ${conf.text}</span>${hasItems?`<button class="food-breakdown-toggle" onclick="toggleFoodBreakdown(${log.id})">+ detalii AI</button>`:''}</div>
        ${hasItems?`<div class="food-breakdown" id="fbd-${log.id}" style="display:none">${bdHtml}<p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:8px;line-height:1.5">${log.notes?escapeHtml(log.notes):'Estimare AI bazată pe porții românești standard.'}</p></div>`:''}
    </div>`;
}
function toggleFoodBreakdown(logId) {
    const bd=document.getElementById(`fbd-${logId}`); const btn=document.querySelector(`#flog-${logId} .food-breakdown-toggle`);
    if(!bd) return; const o=bd.style.display==='none'; bd.style.display=o?'block':'none'; if(btn) btn.textContent=o?'− ascunde':'+ detalii AI';
}
async function deleteFood(logId) {
    const token=localStorage.getItem('coaching_token'); if(!token) return;
    const item=document.getElementById(`flog-${logId}`);
    if(item){item.style.opacity='0.35';item.style.pointerEvents='none';}
    try {
        const res=await fetch(`/food/log/${logId}`,{method:'DELETE',headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`}});
        if(res.ok){showToast('✓ Log șters');await loadFoodToday();}
        else{if(item){item.style.opacity='1';item.style.pointerEvents='auto';}showToast('⚠ Nu s-a putut șterge',true);}
    } catch{if(item){item.style.opacity='1';item.style.pointerEvents='auto';}}
}
function showFoodError(msg){const el=document.getElementById('foodInputError');el.textContent=`⚠  ${msg}`;el.style.display='block';}
function hideFoodError(){document.getElementById('foodInputError').style.display='none';}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// #16 · EXERCISE LOGGER
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// POST /exercise/log  → parse AI (MET × kg × ore = kcal)
// GET  /exercise/today → sumar zilnic
// DELETE /exercise/log/{id}
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

let _selectedExType = '';

function selectExType(type) {
    _selectedExType = type;
    document.querySelectorAll('.ex-type-btn').forEach(b =>
        b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.extype === type));
}

// ── POST /exercise/log ────────────────────────────────────────
async function logExercise() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) { showExError('Autentifică-te mai întâi.'); return; }

    const input = document.getElementById('exerciseInput');
    const desc  = input.value.trim();
    if (desc.length < 3) { showExError('Descrie antrenamentul (minim 3 caractere).'); return; }

    const btn     = document.getElementById('btnExLog');
    const spinner = document.getElementById('exSpinner');
    const btnText = document.getElementById('exLogBtnText');
    btn.disabled  = true;
    spinner.style.display = 'block';
    btnText.style.display = 'none';
    hideExError();

    try {
        const res = await fetch('/exercise/log', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` },
            body:    JSON.stringify({ description: desc, exercise_type: _selectedExType }),
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || 'Eroare la logare.');

        input.value = '';
        input.style.height = '';
        await loadExerciseToday();
        showToast(`✓ ${data.parsed.exercise_name} · ${data.parsed.calories_burned} kcal arse · ${data.parsed.duration_min} min`);
    } catch(e) {
        showExError(e.message);
    } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        spinner.style.display = 'none';
        btnText.style.display = '';
    }
}

// ── GET /exercise/today ───────────────────────────────────────
async function loadExerciseToday(attempt = 1) {
    const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 3;
    const RETRY_DELAY  = 1500;
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    try {
        const res = await fetch('/exercise/today', {
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
        });
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Eroare server');
        const data = await res.json();

        const card = document.getElementById('exProgressCard');
        const list = document.getElementById('exerciseLogsList');

        if (data.logs.length === 0) {
            card.style.display = 'none';
            list.innerHTML = '';
            return;
        }

        // ── Sumar ──────────────────────────────────────────────
        card.style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('exCalBurned').textContent    = data.summary.calories_burned;
        document.getElementById('exMinutesTotal').textContent = data.summary.total_minutes;

        // Net caloric dacă există și food progress
        const foodConsumedEl = document.getElementById('foodCalConsumed');
        if (foodConsumedEl && foodConsumedEl.textContent !== '0') {
            const consumed = parseInt(foodConsumedEl.textContent) || 0;
            const burned   = data.summary.calories_burned;
            const net      = consumed - burned;
            document.getElementById('exNetKcal').textContent = net.toLocaleString('ro');
            document.getElementById('exNetCalBlock').style.display = 'flex';
        } else {
            document.getElementById('exNetCalBlock').style.display = 'none';
        }

        // ── Lista loguri ───────────────────────────────────────
        list.innerHTML = data.logs.map(log => `
            <div class="ex-log-item" id="exlog-${log.id}">
                <div class="ex-log-left">
                    <div class="ex-log-name">${exTypeIcon(log.exercise_type)} ${_esc(log.exercise_name)}</div>
                    <div class="ex-log-meta">
                        ${log.duration_min} min &nbsp;·&nbsp; MET ${log.met_value}
                        &nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="ex-intensity-pill ex-int-${log.intensity}">${intensityLabel(log.intensity)}</span>
                        ${log.notes ? `<br><span style="color:var(--text-muted);font-size:11px">${_esc(log.notes)}</span>` : ''}
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div class="ex-log-right">
                    <span class="ex-log-kcal">-${log.calories_burned}<small>kcal</small></span>
                    <button class="btn-ex-delete" onclick="deleteExerciseLog(${log.id})" title="Șterge">✕</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `).join('');
    } catch(e) {
        if (attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
            setTimeout(() => loadExerciseToday(attempt + 1), RETRY_DELAY);
        } else {
            console.error('loadExerciseToday după', MAX_ATTEMPTS, 'încercări:', e);
        }
    }
}

// ── DELETE /exercise/log/{id} ─────────────────────────────────
async function deleteExerciseLog(id) {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/exercise/log/${id}`, {
            method: 'DELETE',
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
        });
        if (res.ok) { showToast('✓ Log șters'); await loadExerciseToday(); }
    } catch(e) { console.error('deleteExerciseLog:', e); }
}

// ── Utilitare Exercise ────────────────────────────────────────
function exTypeIcon(type) {
    const icons = { cardio:'🏃', forta:'💪', flexibilitate:'🧘', sport:'⚽', general:'🏋️' };
    return icons[type] || '🏋️';
}
function intensityLabel(intensity) {
    const labels = { usor:'Ușor', moderat:'Moderat', intens:'Intens', very_intens:'Foarte intens' };
    return labels[intensity] || intensity;
}
function showExError(msg) {
    const el = document.getElementById('exerciseInputError');
    el.textContent = `⚠  ${msg}`; el.style.display = 'block';
}
function hideExError() {
    document.getElementById('exerciseInputError').style.display = 'none';
}
// _esc() poate exista deja în cod — definit safe dacă lipsește
if (typeof _esc !== 'function') {
    function _esc(s) {
        return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
    }
}
function escapeHtml(str){if(!str)return'';return str.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');}


// ── P1 · VOICE LOGGER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Pipeline: MediaRecorder → WebM audio → POST /food/voice
//           → Groq Whisper Large v3 → text transcris
//           → Groq Llama 3.3 70B → macros → food_logs → UI update
// Target: <5 secunde end-to-end. Zero cost operațional (Groq free tier).
let _vRec=null,_vChunks=[],_vTimer=null,_vSecs=0;
async function toggleVoiceRecording() {
    if (!_isPremium) { showPremiumUpsell('Voice Logging'); return; }
    if (_vRec && _vRec.state === 'recording') { _vStop(); } else { await _vStart(); }
}
async function _vStart(){
    const token=localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if(!token){showFoodError('Autentifică-te pentru a folosi voice logging.');return;}
    hideFoodError();
    let stream;
    try{stream=await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio:true,video:false});}
    catch(err){showFoodError(err.name==='NotAllowedError'?'⚠ Permite accesul la microfon în setările browserului.':`⚠ Microfon indisponibil: ${err.message}`);return;}
    const mime=['audio/webm;codecs=opus','audio/webm','audio/mp4','audio/ogg'].find(t=>MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(t))||'';
    _vChunks=[];_vSecs=0;
    _vRec=new MediaRecorder(stream,mime?{mimeType:mime}:undefined);
    _vRec.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.size>0)_vChunks.push(e.data);};
    _vRec.onstop=async()=>{stream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());await _vProcess();};
    _vRec.start(200);
    const btn=document.getElementById('btnVoice');
    btn.classList.add('recording');btn.textContent='⏹';btn.title='Click pentru a opri înregistrarea';
    document.getElementById('voiceStatusRow').style.display='flex';
    document.getElementById('voiceDot').style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('voiceStatusText').textContent='Înregistrare...';
    document.getElementById('voiceTimer').textContent='0s';
    _vTimer=setInterval(()=>{_vSecs++;document.getElementById('voiceTimer').textContent=`${_vSecs}s`;if(_vSecs>=30)_vStop();},1000);
}
function _vStop(){
    clearInterval(_vTimer);
    if(_vRec&&_vRec.state==='recording')_vRec.stop();
    const btn=document.getElementById('btnVoice');
    btn.classList.remove('recording');btn.classList.add('processing');btn.textContent='⏳';btn.title='Se procesează...';
    document.getElementById('voiceDot').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('voiceStatusText').textContent='🎙 Transcriere cu Whisper...';
}
async function _vProcess(){
    const token=localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if(!_vChunks.length||!token){_vReset();return;}
    const mime=_vChunks[0].type||'audio/webm';
    const ext=mime.includes('mp4')?'m4a':mime.includes('ogg')?'ogg':'webm';
    const blob=new Blob(_vChunks,{type:mime});
    if(blob.size<1000){showFoodError('⚠ Înregistrarea e prea scurtă. Vorbește mai mult și încearcă din nou.');_vReset();return;}
    const fd=new FormData();
    fd.append('audio',blob,`voice.${ext}`);
    fd.append('meal_type',selectedMealType);
    try{
        const res=await fetch('/food/voice',{method:'POST',headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`},body:fd});
        const data=await res.json();
        if(!res.ok){showFoodError(data.detail||'⚠ Eroare la procesare audio.');_vReset();return;}
        const inp=document.getElementById('foodInput');
        inp.value=data.transcription;
        inp.style.borderColor='var(--accent)';inp.style.boxShadow='0 0 0 4px var(--accent-glow)';
        setTimeout(()=>{inp.style.borderColor='';inp.style.boxShadow='';},2200);
        const shortT=data.transcription.length>48?data.transcription.substring(0,48)+'…':data.transcription;
        document.getElementById('voiceStatusText').textContent=`✓ "${shortT}"`;
        showToast(`🎤 Logat vocal: ${data.parsed?.totals?.calories||0} kcal · ${FOOD_MEAL_LABELS[selectedMealType]||'General'}`);
        await loadFoodToday();
        setTimeout(()=>{inp.value='';inp.style.height='auto';},1800);
    }catch(e){showFoodError(`⚠ Eroare rețea: ${e.message}`);}
    finally{setTimeout(_vReset,2800);}
}
function _vReset(){
    const btn=document.getElementById('btnVoice');
    btn.classList.remove('recording','processing');
    btn.textContent='🎤';btn.title='Logare prin voce — click pentru a înregistra';
    document.getElementById('voiceStatusRow').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('voiceStatusText').textContent='Înregistrare...';
    document.getElementById('voiceTimer').textContent='0s';
    _vRec=null;_vChunks=[];
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  PHOTO FOOD LOG ENGINE
//  Pipeline: Camera → Canvas compress → base64 → POST /food/photo/analyze
//            → Groq Llama 4 Scout Vision → macros → confirm → POST /food/photo/log
//  Target: <5 secunde end-to-end. Exclusiv Premium.
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

let _photoAnalysisData = null;   // date analizate — salvate pentru confirm
let _photoCurrentFile  = null;   // fișierul original — pentru retry

// ── Deschide Photo Logger ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
function openPhotoFoodLogger() {
    if (!_isPremium) { showPremiumUpsell('Photo Food Log'); return; }
    document.getElementById('photoInput').click();
}

// ── Handler selecție fișier ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
function handlePhotoSelected(input) {
    const file = input.files && input.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    input.value = '';   // reset pentru a permite re-selecție același fișier
    _photoCurrentFile = file;
    _startPhotoFlow(file);
}

// ── Flow principal ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function _startPhotoFlow(file) {
    _photoAnalysisData = null;
    _openPhotoModal();
    _showPhotoPreview(file);
    _setPhotoStatus('Se comprimă imaginea...', 'Compresie');
    _showPhotoScanLine(true);

    let base64;
    try {
        base64 = await _compressImage(file, 820, 0.82);
    } catch (err) {
        _setPhotoStatus('Eroare la procesarea imaginii.', 'Eroare');
        _showPhotoScanLine(false);
        return;
    }

    _setPhotoStatus('AI analizează alimentele...', 'Analiză Groq Vision');
    await _analyzePhoto(base64);
}

// ── Deschide modal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

}

// ── Scan line animation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    if (!sl) return;
    sl.classList.toggle('active', show);
}

// ── Compresie imagine client-side (Canvas API) ───────────────────────────────
function _compressImage(file, maxPx, quality) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const img = new Image();
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
        img.onload = () => {
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            let w = img.naturalWidth, h = img.naturalHeight;
            if (w > maxPx || h > maxPx) {
                if (w > h) { h = Math.round(h * maxPx / w); w = maxPx; }
                else        { w = Math.round(w * maxPx / h); h = maxPx; }
            }
            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            canvas.width  = w;
            canvas.height = h;
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
            ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h);
            const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', quality);
            const approxKb = Math.round(dataUrl.length * 0.75 / 1024);
            console.log(`Photo compress: ${img.naturalWidth}×${img.naturalHeight} → ${w}×${h} · ~${approxKb}KB`);
            resolve(dataUrl);
        };
        img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); reject(new Error('Nu s-a putut încărca imaginea.')); };
        img.src = url;
    });
}

// ── Analiză AI ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function _analyzePhoto(base64DataUrl) {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) { closePhotoModal(); return; }

    try {
        const res = await fetch('/food/photo/analyze', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
            body: JSON.stringify({ image_base64: base64DataUrl, meal_type: selectedMealType }),
        });
        const data = await res.json();

        _showPhotoScanLine(false);
        document.getElementById('photoStatusRow').style.display = 'none';

        if (!res.ok) {
            const msg = data.detail?.message || data.detail || 'Eroare la analiză.';
            showToast(`⚠ ${msg}`, true);
            closePhotoModal();
            return;
        }

        _photoAnalysisData = data;

        if (!data.detected) {
            _showNotDetected();
        } else {
            _renderPhotoResults(data);
        }

    } catch (err) {
        _showPhotoScanLine(false);
        document.getElementById('photoStatusRow').style.display = 'none';
        showToast('⚠ Eroare de conexiune. Verifică internetul și încearcă din nou.', true);
        closePhotoModal();
    }
}

// ── Render rezultate ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _renderPhotoResults(data) {
    const qBadge  = document.getElementById('photoQualityBadge');
    const qLabels  = { high:'Precizie Mare', medium:'Precizie Medie', low:'Estimare' };
    const qKey     = data.analysis_quality || 'medium';
    qBadge.textContent  = qLabels[qKey] || 'Estimare';
    qBadge.className    = `photo-quality-badge ${qKey}`;

    document.getElementById('photoMealName').textContent = data.meal_name || 'Masă detectată';

    const list = document.getElementById('photoFoodsList');
    list.innerHTML = (data.foods || []).map(f => {
        const confLabels = { high: '● Sigur', medium: '● Incert', low: '● Aproximativ' };
        return `<div class="detected-food-item">
            <div class="dfi-row1">
                <div>
                    <div class="dfi-name">${escapeHtml(f.name)}</div>
                    <div class="dfi-portion">${escapeHtml(f.portion_estimate || '—')}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="dfi-kcal">${f.calories}<small>kcal</small></div>
            </div>
            <div class="dfi-macros">
                <span class="dfi-pill prot">P: ${f.protein_g}g</span>
                <span class="dfi-pill carb">C: ${f.carbs_g}g</span>
                <span class="dfi-pill fat">G: ${f.fat_g}g</span>
                <span class="dfi-confidence ${f.confidence || 'medium'}">${confLabels[f.confidence] || '● Mediu'}</span>
            </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');

    document.getElementById('photoTotalKcal').textContent = data.total_calories  || 0;
    document.getElementById('photoTotalProt').textContent = `${data.total_protein_g || 0}g`;
    document.getElementById('photoTotalCarb').textContent = `${data.total_carbs_g  || 0}g`;
    document.getElementById('photoTotalFat').textContent  = `${data.total_fat_g    || 0}g`;

    const notesEl = document.getElementById('photoNotes');
    if (data.notes) { notesEl.textContent = data.notes; notesEl.style.display = 'block'; }
    else              { notesEl.style.display = 'none'; }

    const btnLog = document.getElementById('btnPhotoLog');
    btnLog.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:16px;height:16px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg> Loghează totul · ${data.total_calories} kcal`;

    document.getElementById('photoNotDetected').style.display  = 'none';
    document.getElementById('photoResultsPanel').style.display = 'block';
}

// ── Not detected state ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _showNotDetected() {
    document.getElementById('photoResultsPanel').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('photoFoodsList').innerHTML        = '';
    document.getElementById('photoMealName').textContent       = '';
    document.getElementById('photoActions').style.display      = 'none';
    document.getElementById('photoNotDetected').style.display  = 'block';
}

// ── Confirmă și loghează ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function confirmPhotoLog() {
    if (!_photoAnalysisData || !_photoAnalysisData.detected) return;
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const btn = document.getElementById('btnPhotoLog');
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.innerHTML = '<div class="food-spinner" style="display:inline-block;width:14px;height:14px;border-width:2px;border-top-color:#fff;margin-right:8px;animation:spin .7s linear infinite;border-radius:50%;border-style:solid;border-color:rgba(255,255,255,0.3);border-top-color:#fff"></div>Se loghează...';

    try {
        const payload = {
            meal_type:        selectedMealType,
            meal_name:        _photoAnalysisData.meal_name,
            foods:            _photoAnalysisData.foods,
            total_calories:   _photoAnalysisData.total_calories,
            total_protein_g:  _photoAnalysisData.total_protein_g,
            total_carbs_g:    _photoAnalysisData.total_carbs_g,
            total_fat_g:      _photoAnalysisData.total_fat_g,
            analysis_quality: _photoAnalysisData.analysis_quality,
        };

        const res  = await fetch('/food/photo/log', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
            body:    JSON.stringify(payload),
        });
        const data = await res.json();

        if (!res.ok) {
            showToast(`⚠ ${data.detail?.message || data.detail || 'Eroare la logare.'}`, true);
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:16px;height:16px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg> Loghează totul · ${_photoAnalysisData.total_calories} kcal`;
            return;
        }

        document.getElementById('photoResultsPanel').style.display  = 'none';
        const successEl = document.getElementById('photoSuccessState');
        successEl.style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('photoSuccessTitle').textContent    = `${data.total_calories} kcal logate!`;
        document.getElementById('photoSuccessSub').textContent      = `${(_photoAnalysisData.foods || []).length} alimente · ${FOOD_MEAL_LABELS[data.meal_type] || 'General'}`;

        const foodN = _achBump('nl_food_count');
        _checkAchievements('food', foodN);

        showToast(`✓ Photo Log: ${data.total_calories} kcal · ${FOOD_MEAL_LABELS[data.meal_type] || 'General'}`);
        await loadFoodToday();

        setTimeout(closePhotoModal, 2000);

    } catch (err) {
        showToast('⚠ Eroare de conexiune.', true);
        btn.disabled = false;
    }
}

// ── Retry (re-deschide camera) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
function retryPhotoAnalysis() {
    if (_photoCurrentFile) {
        _photoAnalysisData = null;
        document.getElementById('photoResultsPanel').style.display  = 'none';
        document.getElementById('photoSuccessState').style.display  = 'none';
        document.getElementById('photoPreviewImg').style.display    = 'none';
        document.getElementById('photoStatusRow').style.display     = 'none';
        document.getElementById('photoActions').style.display       = 'flex';
        document.getElementById('photoInput').click();
    } else {
        closePhotoModal();
        document.getElementById('photoInput').click();
    }
}

// ── Închide modal ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    const modal = document.getElementById('photoModal');
    modal.classList.remove('open');
    document.body.style.overflow = '';
    _photoAnalysisData = null;
    document.getElementById('photoResultsPanel').style.display  = 'none';
    document.getElementById('photoSuccessState').style.display  = 'none';
    document.getElementById('photoStatusRow').style.display     = 'none';
    document.getElementById('photoActions').style.display       = 'flex';
    _showPhotoScanLine(false);
}

        closePhotoModal();
    }
});

//  FRIDGE PLANNER ENGINE
//  Flow: Foto/Text → POST /food/fridge → plan 4 mese → log individual
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

let _fridgeCurrentTab    = 'photo';
let _fridgePhotoBase64   = null;
let _fridgePlanData      = null;

const FRIDGE_MEAL_LABELS = {
    mic_dejun: 'Mic Dejun',
    pranz:     'Prânz',
    gustare:   'Gustare',
    cina:      'Cină',
};
const FRIDGE_MEAL_ORDER = ['mic_dejun', 'pranz', 'gustare', 'cina'];

// ── Deschide modal ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function openFridgePlanner() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('fridgeModal');
    modal.classList.add('open');
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    resetFridgeModal(false);
}

function closeFridgeModal() {
    document.getElementById('fridgeModal').classList.remove('open');
    document.body.style.overflow = '';
    _fridgePhotoBase64 = null;
    _fridgePlanData    = null;
}

// ESC
document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && document.getElementById('fridgeModal').classList.contains('open')) {
        closeFridgeModal();
    }
});

// ── Tabs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function switchFridgeTab(tab) {
    _fridgeCurrentTab = tab;
    document.getElementById('fridgePhotoSection').style.display = tab === 'photo' ? 'block' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('fridgeTextSection').style.display  = tab === 'text'  ? 'block' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('fridgeTabPhoto').classList.toggle('active', tab === 'photo');
    document.getElementById('fridgeTabText').classList.toggle('active',  tab === 'text');
}

// ── Foto frigider ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function handleFridgePhoto(input) {
    const file = input.files && input.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    input.value = '';

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
        _fridgePhotoBase64 = e.target.result;
        const preview = document.getElementById('fridgePreviewImg');
        preview.src   = e.target.result;
        preview.style.display = 'block';
        document.querySelector('.fridge-photo-wrap').style.display = 'none';
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

// ── Analiză + generare plan ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function analyzeFridge() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) { showToast('⚠ Autentifică-te mai întâi.', true); return; }

    const textInput = document.getElementById('fridgeIngredientsInput').value.trim();

    if (!_fridgePhotoBase64 && !textInput) {
        showToast('⚠ Fotografiază frigiderul sau listează ingredientele.', true);
        return;
    }

    // UI loading
    const btn     = document.getElementById('btnFridgeAnalyze');
    const btnText = document.getElementById('fridgeAnalyzeBtnText');
    const spinner = document.getElementById('fridgeSpinner');
    btn.disabled  = true;
    btnText.textContent = 'Se generează planul...';
    spinner.style.display = 'block';

    document.getElementById('fridgeStatus').style.display    = 'flex';
    document.getElementById('fridgeStatusText').textContent  = _fridgePhotoBase64
        ? 'AI detectează ingredientele din frigider...'
        : 'Se construiește planul zilei...';
    document.getElementById('fridgeResultPanel').style.display      = 'none';
    document.getElementById('fridgeIngredientsWrap').style.display  = 'none';

    try {
        const payload = {};
        if (_fridgePhotoBase64) payload.image_base64      = _fridgePhotoBase64;
        if (textInput)          payload.ingredients_text  = textInput;

        const res  = await fetch('/food/fridge', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
            body:    JSON.stringify(payload),
        });
        const data = await res.json();

        if (!res.ok) {
            const msg = data.detail?.message || data.detail || 'Eroare la generare.';
            showToast(`⚠ ${msg}`, true);
            return;
        }

        _fridgePlanData = data;
        _renderFridgePlan(data);

    } catch (e) {
        showToast('⚠ Eroare de conexiune. Încearcă din nou.', true);
    } finally {
        document.getElementById('fridgeStatus').style.display = 'none';
        btn.disabled = false;
        btnText.textContent = '✦ Generează planul zilei mele';
        spinner.style.display = 'none';
    }
}

// ── Render rezultate ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _renderFridgePlan(data) {
    // Ingrediente detectate
    const ings = data.ingredients_found || [];
    if (ings.length) {
        const wrap = document.getElementById('fridgeIngredientsWrap');
        const list = document.getElementById('fridgeIngredientsList');
        wrap.style.display = 'block';
        list.innerHTML = ings.map(i =>
            `<span class="fridge-ing-chip">${escapeHtml(i)}</span>`
        ).join('');
    }

    // Cărțile meselor
    const plan     = data.plan || {};
    const cardsEl  = document.getElementById('fridgeMealCards');
    cardsEl.innerHTML = FRIDGE_MEAL_ORDER.map(key => {
        const m = plan[key];
        if (!m) return '';
        return `<div class="fridge-meal-card">
            <div class="fridge-meal-header">
                <div>
                    <div class="fridge-meal-label">${escapeHtml(m.label || FRIDGE_MEAL_LABELS[key])}</div>
                    <div class="fridge-meal-name">${escapeHtml(m.name || '—')}</div>
                    <div class="fridge-meal-prep">⏱ ${escapeHtml(m.prep_time || '—')}</div>
                </div>
                <div class="fridge-meal-kcal">${m.calories || 0}<small>kcal</small></div>
            </div>
            <div class="fridge-meal-desc">${escapeHtml(m.description || '')}</div>
            <div class="fridge-meal-macros">
                <span class="fridge-pill p">P: ${m.protein_g || 0}g</span>
                <span class="fridge-pill c">C: ${m.carbs_g   || 0}g</span>
                <span class="fridge-pill g">G: ${m.fat_g     || 0}g</span>
            </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join('');

    // Totaluri
    document.getElementById('fridgeTotalKcal').textContent = data.total_calories  || 0;
    document.getElementById('fridgeTotalProt').textContent = `${data.total_protein_g || 0}g`;
    document.getElementById('fridgeTotalCarb').textContent = `${data.total_carbs_g   || 0}g`;
    document.getElementById('fridgeTotalFat').textContent  = `${data.total_fat_g     || 0}g`;
    document.getElementById('fridgeTotalBadge').textContent = `${data.total_calories || 0} kcal`;

    // Note
    const notesEl = document.getElementById('fridgeNotes');
    if (data.notes) {
        notesEl.textContent  = data.notes;
        notesEl.style.display = 'block';
    } else {
        notesEl.style.display = 'none';
    }

    // Buton log
    document.getElementById('btnFridgeLog').innerHTML =
        `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:16px;height:16px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg> Loghează toate mesele · ${data.total_calories || 0} kcal`;

    document.getElementById('fridgeResultPanel').style.display = 'block';
}

// ── Loghează toate mesele din plan ────────────────────────────────────────────
async function logFridgeMeals() {
    if (!_fridgePlanData || !_fridgePlanData.plan) return;
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const btn = document.getElementById('btnFridgeLog');
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.innerHTML = '<div class="food-spinner" style="display:inline-block;width:14px;height:14px;border-width:2px;border-radius:50%;border-style:solid;border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.3);border-top-color:#fff;animation:spin .7s linear infinite;margin-right:8px"></div>Se loghează...';

    const plan   = _fridgePlanData.plan;
    const errors = [];

    for (const [mealKey, meal] of Object.entries(plan)) {
        if (!meal || !meal.calories) continue;
        try {
            const desc = `${meal.name} — ${meal.description || ''}`.trim().replace(/—\s*$/, '');
            await fetch('/food/log', {
                method:  'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    description: desc,
                    meal_type:   mealKey,
                }),
            });
        } catch {
            errors.push(mealKey);
        }
    }

    if (errors.length === 0) {
        showToast(`✓ Fridge Plan logat: ${_fridgePlanData.total_calories} kcal · 4 mese`);
        const foodN = _achBump('nl_food_count');
        _checkAchievements('food', foodN);
        await loadFoodToday();
        setTimeout(closeFridgeModal, 1500);
    } else {
        showToast(`⚠ Unele mese nu s-au logat. Încearcă din nou.`, true);
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:16px;height:16px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2;stroke-linecap:round;stroke-linejoin:round"><polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12"/></svg> Loghează toate mesele · ${_fridgePlanData.total_calories || 0} kcal`;
    }
}

// ── Reset modal ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function resetFridgeModal(keepOpen = true) {
    _fridgePhotoBase64 = null;
    _fridgePlanData    = null;

    document.getElementById('fridgeResultPanel').style.display     = 'none';
    document.getElementById('fridgeIngredientsWrap').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('fridgeStatus').style.display          = 'none';
    document.getElementById('fridgePreviewImg').style.display      = 'none';
    document.querySelector('.fridge-photo-wrap').style.display     = 'block';
    document.getElementById('fridgeIngredientsInput').value        = '';
    document.getElementById('fridgeIngredientsList').innerHTML     = '';
    document.getElementById('fridgeMealCards').innerHTML           = '';

    const btn     = document.getElementById('btnFridgeAnalyze');
    const btnText = document.getElementById('fridgeAnalyzeBtnText');
    btn.disabled  = false;
    btnText.textContent = '✦ Generează planul zilei mele';

    switchFridgeTab('photo');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  #15 · BARCODE SCANNER ENGINE
//  Pipeline: Camera → BarcodeDetector API → GET /food/barcode/{code}
//            → Open Food Facts → macros → confirm → POST /food/log
//
//  BarcodeDetector: nativ în Chrome 83+, Edge 83+, Samsung Browser 14+
//  Fallback: câmp de intrare manuală (pentru Firefox, Safari vechi)
//  Open Food Facts: API gratuit, zero-auth, 3M+ produse
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
let _isPremium = false;
let _barcodeStream  = null;   // MediaStream activ
let _barcodeLoop    = null;   // requestAnimationFrame handle
let _barcodeDetector = null;  // BarcodeDetector instance
let _barcodeData    = null;   // produsul curent ({name, brand, per100g macros})
let _barcodeLastCode = null;  // ultimul cod detectat (anti-spam)

// ── Deschide modalul ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function openBarcodeScanner() {
    if (!_isPremium) { showPremiumUpsell('Barcode Scanner'); return; }
    const modal = document.getElementById('barcodeModal');
    modal.style.display = 'flex';
    _resetBarcodeUI();

    // Verifică suport BarcodeDetector
    const hasBD = ('BarcodeDetector' in window);

    if (!hasBD) {
        // Fallback: input manual
        document.getElementById('barcodeVideoWrap').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('barcodeManualWrap').style.display = 'block';
        return;
    }

    // Inițializează BarcodeDetector cu formatele EAN/UPC principale
    try {
        _barcodeDetector = new BarcodeDetector({
            formats: ['ean_13', 'ean_8', 'upc_a', 'upc_e', 'code_128']
        });
    } catch {
        _barcodeDetector = new BarcodeDetector();  // detectează orice format
    }

    // Pornește camera (back camera pe mobil)
    try {
        _barcodeStream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
            video: { facingMode: { ideal: 'environment' }, width: { ideal: 1280 } },
            audio: false,
        });
    } catch (err) {
        document.getElementById('barcodeVideoWrap').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('barcodeManualWrap').style.display = 'block';
        return;
    }

    const video = document.getElementById('barcodeVideo');
    video.srcObject = _barcodeStream;
    await video.play();

    // Pornește loop-ul de detecție
    _barcodeScanLoop(video);
}

// ── Scan loop (rAF pentru CPU minim) ─────────────────────────────────────
function _barcodeScanLoop(video) {
    if (!_barcodeDetector || !_barcodeStream) return;

    _barcodeLoop = requestAnimationFrame(async () => {
        try {
            const codes = await _barcodeDetector.detect(video);
            if (codes && codes.length > 0) {
                const code = codes[0].rawValue;
                if (code && code !== _barcodeLastCode) {
                    _barcodeLastCode = code;
                    await onBarcodeDetected(code);
                    return; // Oprire loop după detecție
                }
            }
        } catch { /* frame poate fi invalid la pornire */ }

        // Continuă scanarea
        if (_barcodeStream && _barcodeStream.active) {
            _barcodeScanLoop(video);
        }
    });
}

// ── Cod detectat → lookup produs ──────────────────────────────────────────
async function onBarcodeDetected(code) {
    // Feedback vizual imediat
    document.getElementById('barcodeScanStatus').textContent = `✓ Cod detectat: ${code}`;
    document.getElementById('barcodeScanStatus').style.color = 'var(--accent)';

    // Oprește camera (economie baterie)
    _stopBarcodeCamera();

    await _fetchBarcodeProduct(code);
}

async function lookupBarcodeManual() {
    const inp = document.getElementById('barcodeManualInput');
    const code = inp.value.trim();
    if (!code || code.length < 7) {
        inp.style.borderColor = 'rgba(239,68,68,0.6)';
        setTimeout(() => inp.style.borderColor = '', 1500);
        return;
    }
    await _fetchBarcodeProduct(code);
}

// ── Fetch produs de la backend ────────────────────────────────────────────
async function _fetchBarcodeProduct(code) {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) { _showBarcodeError('Autentifică-te pentru a folosi barcode scanner.'); return; }

    _showBarcodeSpinner(true);
    _hideBarcodeError();
    document.getElementById('barcodeResultCard').style.display = 'none';

    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/food/barcode/${code}?grams=100`, {
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
        });
        const data = await res.json();

        if (res.status === 402) {
            closeBarcodeScanner();
            showPremiumUpsell('Barcode Scanner');
            return;
        }

        if (!res.ok) {
            _showBarcodeError(data.detail || `Produs negăsit (cod: ${code}). Descrie manual mâncarea.`);
            _showBarcodeManualFallback();
            return;
        }

        _barcodeData = data;  // stochează per-100g
        _renderBarcodeProduct(data);

    } catch (err) {
        _showBarcodeError(`Eroare de rețea: ${err.message}`);
    } finally {
        _showBarcodeSpinner(false);
    }
}

// ── Randare produs ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _renderBarcodeProduct(data) {
    // Imagine
    const img = document.getElementById('barcodeProductImg');
    if (data.image_url) {
        img.src = data.image_url;
        img.style.display = 'block';
        img.onerror = () => { img.style.display = 'none'; };
    } else {
        img.style.display = 'none';
    }

    // Nume și brand
    document.getElementById('barcodeProductName').textContent = data.name;
    const brandEl = document.getElementById('barcodeProductBrand');
    brandEl.textContent = [data.brand, data.quantity].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');

    // Macros per 100g (label-uri)
    const grid = document.getElementById('barcodeMacrosGrid');
    grid.innerHTML = _macroMiniCard('kcal/100g', data.calories, '#d4d0cb')
        + _macroMiniCard('prot g/100g', data.protein_g, '#e07b45')
        + _macroMiniCard('carb g/100g', data.carbs_g, '#5b9cf6')
        + _macroMiniCard('grăs g/100g', data.fat_g, '#f0c060');

    // Porție sugerată
    const servBadge = document.getElementById('barcodeServing');
    if (data.serving_g) {
        document.getElementById('barcodeGramsInput').value = data.serving_g;
        servBadge.textContent = `↑ porție sugerată: ${data.serving_g}g`;
        servBadge.style.display = 'inline';
    } else {
        document.getElementById('barcodeGramsInput').value = 100;
        servBadge.style.display = 'none';
    }

    recalcBarcodeMacros();

    document.getElementById('barcodeResultCard').style.display = 'block';
}

function _macroMiniCard(label, val, color) {
    return `<div style="background:var(--surface-2);border:1px solid var(--border);
                border-radius:8px;padding:8px 6px;text-align:center;">
              <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:${color};">${val}</div>
              <div style="font-size:9px;color:var(--text-secondary);margin-top:2px;
                          letter-spacing:0.5px;">${label}</div>
            </div>`;
}

// ── Recalcul macros pentru porția selectată ───────────────────────────────
function recalcBarcodeMacros() {
    if (!_barcodeData) return;
    const grams = parseFloat(document.getElementById('barcodeGramsInput').value) || 100;
    const f = grams / 100;
    const kcal = Math.round(_barcodeData.calories  * f);
    const prot = Math.round(_barcodeData.protein_g * f * 10) / 10;
    const carb = Math.round(_barcodeData.carbs_g   * f * 10) / 10;
    const fat  = Math.round(_barcodeData.fat_g     * f * 10) / 10;
    document.getElementById('brKcal').textContent = kcal;
    document.getElementById('brProt').textContent = prot;
    document.getElementById('brCarb').textContent = carb;
    document.getElementById('brFat').textContent  = fat;
}

// ── Confirmă și log ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function confirmBarcodeProduct() {
    if (!_barcodeData) return;

    const grams = parseFloat(document.getElementById('barcodeGramsInput').value) || 100;
    const f     = grams / 100;
    const kcal  = Math.round(_barcodeData.calories  * f);
    const prot  = (Math.round(_barcodeData.protein_g * f * 10) / 10);
    const carb  = (Math.round(_barcodeData.carbs_g   * f * 10) / 10);
    const fat   = (Math.round(_barcodeData.fat_g     * f * 10) / 10);

    // Construim descrierea pentru food_log (compatibil cu POST /food/log)
    const nameStr  = _barcodeData.brand ? `${_barcodeData.name} ${_barcodeData.brand}` : _barcodeData.name;
    const description = `${grams}g ${nameStr} — ${kcal} kcal, proteină ${prot}g, carb ${carb}g, grăsimi ${fat}g`;

    // Închidem modalul
    closeBarcodeScanner();

    // Populăm textarea și trimitem direct
    const inp = document.getElementById('foodInput');
    inp.value = description;
    inp.style.height = 'auto';
    inp.style.height = Math.min(inp.scrollHeight, 120) + 'px';

    // Flash vizual
    inp.style.borderColor = 'var(--accent)';
    inp.style.boxShadow   = '0 0 0 4px var(--accent-glow)';
    setTimeout(() => { inp.style.borderColor = ''; inp.style.boxShadow = ''; }, 1800);

    showToast(`📷 Produs scanat: ${_barcodeData.name} · ${kcal} kcal`);

    // Submit automat
    await logFood();
}

// ── Închide scanner ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function closeBarcodeScanner() {
    _stopBarcodeCamera();
    document.getElementById('barcodeModal').style.display = 'none';
    _resetBarcodeUI();
}

function _stopBarcodeCamera() {
    if (_barcodeLoop) { cancelAnimationFrame(_barcodeLoop); _barcodeLoop = null; }
    if (_barcodeStream) {
        _barcodeStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
        _barcodeStream = null;
    }
    const v = document.getElementById('barcodeVideo');
    if (v) { v.srcObject = null; }
}

// ── Helpers UI ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _resetBarcodeUI() {
    _barcodeData     = null;
    _barcodeLastCode = null;
    document.getElementById('barcodeResultCard').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('barcodeErrorMsg').style.display   = 'none';
    document.getElementById('barcodeLookupSpinner').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('barcodeManualWrap').style.display  = 'none';
    document.getElementById('barcodeVideoWrap').style.display   = 'block';
    document.getElementById('barcodeScanStatus').textContent    = 'Îndreaptă camera spre codul de bare...';
    document.getElementById('barcodeScanStatus').style.color    = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)';
    const inp = document.getElementById('barcodeManualInput');
    if (inp) inp.value = '';
}

function _showBarcodeSpinner(show) {
    document.getElementById('barcodeLookupSpinner').style.display = show ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function _showBarcodeError(msg) {
    const el = document.getElementById('barcodeErrorMsg');
    el.textContent = msg;
    el.style.display = 'block';
}

function _hideBarcodeError() {
    document.getElementById('barcodeErrorMsg').style.display = 'none';
}

function _showBarcodeManualFallback() {
    document.getElementById('barcodeManualWrap').style.display = 'block';
}

// Închide cu Escape
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && document.getElementById('barcodeModal').style.display === 'flex') {
        closeBarcodeScanner();
    }
});

// ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ── ──

const MP_MEAL_ICONS={'Mic Dejun':'🌅','Prânz':'🍽️','Gustare':'☀️','Cină':'🌙'};
async function loadMealPlanSection() {
    const token=localStorage.getItem('coaching_token'); if(!token) return;
    try {
        const res=await fetch('/meal-plan/targets',{headers:{'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`}});
        if(!res.ok) return;
        const data=await res.json();
        if(data.has_targets) {
            document.getElementById('mpTargetsRow').style.display='flex';
            document.getElementById('mpNoTargets').style.display='none';
            document.getElementById('mpTKcal').textContent=data.target_kcal;
            document.getElementById('mpTProt').innerHTML=`${data.protein_g}<small style="font-size:10px;font-weight:400">g</small>`;
            document.getElementById('mpTCarb').innerHTML=`${data.carbs_g}<small style="font-size:10px;font-weight:400">g</small>`;
            document.getElementById('mpTFat').innerHTML=`${data.fat_g}<small style="font-size:10px;font-weight:400">g</small>`;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('mpTargetsRow').style.display='none';
            document.getElementById('mpNoTargets').style.display='block';
            document.getElementById('btnGeneratePlan').disabled=true;
        }
    } catch(e){console.error('MP targets load error:',e);}
}
async function generateMealPlan() {
    const token=localStorage.getItem('coaching_token'); if(!token) return;
    const btn=document.getElementById('btnGeneratePlan'); const btnTxt=document.getElementById('mpBtnText');
    const spinner=document.getElementById('mpSpinner'); const resultsEl=document.getElementById('mpResults');
    btn.disabled=true; btnTxt.textContent='Se generează (15-25 sec)...'; spinner.style.display='block';
    resultsEl.style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('mpDayCards').innerHTML=`<div class="mp-loading"><div class="mp-loading-spinner"></div><div class="mp-loading-title">AI-ul generează planul tău</div><div class="mp-loading-sub">Llama 3.3 70B calculează 28 de mese personalizate<br>cu alimente românești și macros precise...</div></div>`;
    resultsEl.style.display='block';
    try {
        const prefs=document.getElementById('mpPreferences').value.trim();
        const res=await fetch('/meal-plan/generate',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`},body:JSON.stringify({preferences:prefs})});
        const data=await res.json();
        if(!res.ok){document.getElementById('mpDayCards').innerHTML=`<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:#ff7070;font-size:13px;">⚠ ${data.detail||'Eroare la generare. Încearcă din nou.'}</div>`;return;}
        renderMealPlan(data);
        showToast(`✓ Plan generat · ${data.meta?.days_count||7} zile · ${data.meta?.target_kcal} kcal/zi`);
    } catch(e){document.getElementById('mpDayCards').innerHTML=`<div style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:#ff7070;font-size:13px;">⚠ Eroare de conexiune. Verifică că serverul rulează.</div>`;}
    finally{btn.disabled=false;btnTxt.textContent='✦ Generează Planul Meu de 7 Zile';spinner.style.display='none';}
}
function renderMealPlan(data) {
    document.getElementById('mpResults').style.display='block';
    const avg=data.weekly_avg;
    if(avg){
        document.getElementById('mpAvgCal').textContent=avg.calories||'—';
        document.getElementById('mpAvgProt').textContent=avg.protein_g||'—';
        document.getElementById('mpAvgCarb').textContent=avg.carbs_g||'—';
        document.getElementById('mpAvgFat').textContent=avg.fat_g||'—';
    }
    const plan=data.plan||[];
    document.getElementById('mpDayCards').innerHTML=plan.map((day,idx)=>renderMealPlanDay(day,idx)).join('');
    if(plan.length>0){const fc=document.querySelector('.mp-day-card');if(fc)fc.classList.add('active');}
    const tips=data.shopping_tips||[];
    const tipsCard=document.getElementById('mpTipsCard');
    const tipsList=document.getElementById('mpTipsList');
    if(tips.length>0){tipsList.innerHTML=tips.map(tip=>`<div class="mp-tip-item"><div class="mp-tip-dot"></div><span>${escapeHtml(tip)}</span></div>`).join('');tipsCard.style.display='block';}
    else{tipsCard.style.display='none';}
}
function renderMealPlanDay(day,idx) {
    const meals=day.meals||[]; const totals=day.day_totals||{};
    const dayNum=day.day||(idx+1); const dayName=day.day_name||`Ziua ${dayNum}`;
    const mealsHtml=meals.map(meal=>{
        const icon=MP_MEAL_ICONS[meal.meal]||'🍴';
        return `<div class="mp-meal-row"><div class="mp-meal-type">${icon} ${escapeHtml(meal.meal||'')}</div><div class="mp-meal-center"><span class="mp-meal-name">${escapeHtml(meal.name||'—')}</span><span class="mp-meal-desc">${escapeHtml(meal.description||'')}</span><span class="mp-meal-prep">⏱ ${escapeHtml(meal.prep_time||'')}</span></div><div class="mp-meal-right"><span class="mp-meal-kcal">${meal.calories||0}<small>kcal</small></span><div class="mp-meal-macros"><span class="mp-mm p">P${meal.protein_g||0}g</span><span class="mp-mm c">C${meal.carbs_g||0}g</span><span class="mp-mm f">G${meal.fat_g||0}g</span></div></div></div>`;
    }).join('');
    return `<div class="mp-day-card" id="mpDay-${dayNum}"><div class="mp-day-header" onclick="toggleMealPlanDay(${dayNum})"><div class="mp-day-left"><div class="mp-day-num">${dayNum}</div><div><div class="mp-day-name">${escapeHtml(dayName)}</div><div class="mp-day-macros"><span class="mp-day-macro-badge p">P ${totals.protein_g||0}g</span><span class="mp-day-macro-badge" style="color:var(--text-secondary)">·</span><span class="mp-day-macro-badge c">C ${totals.carbs_g||0}g</span><span class="mp-day-macro-badge" style="color:var(--text-secondary)">·</span><span class="mp-day-macro-badge f">G ${totals.fat_g||0}g</span></div></div></div><div class="mp-day-right"><span class="mp-day-kcal">${totals.calories||0}<small>kcal</small></span><span class="mp-day-arrow">▼</span></div></div><div class="mp-day-body">${mealsHtml}</div></div>`;
}
function toggleMealPlanDay(dayNum){const card=document.getElementById(`mpDay-${dayNum}`);if(card)card.classList.toggle('active');}

let chatHistory=[]; let chatWaiting=false;
function toggleChat() {
    const panel=document.getElementById('chatPanel');
    panel.classList.toggle('open');
    if(panel.classList.contains('open')) setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('chatInput').focus(),280);
}
function addBubble(role,text){const c=document.getElementById('chatMessages');const b=document.createElement('div');b.className=`chat-bubble ${role}`;b.textContent=text;c.appendChild(b);c.scrollTop=c.scrollHeight;}
function showTyping(){const c=document.getElementById('chatMessages');const b=document.createElement('div');b.className='chat-bubble ai typing';b.id='typingIndicator';b.innerHTML='<div class="typing-dot"></div><div class="typing-dot"></div><div class="typing-dot"></div>';c.appendChild(b);c.scrollTop=c.scrollHeight;}
async function sendChatMessage() {
    if(chatWaiting) return;
    const input=document.getElementById('chatInput'); const message=input.value.trim(); if(!message) return;
    const token=localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if(!token){addBubble('ai','Trebuie să fii logat pentru a folosi chat-ul.');return;}
    chatWaiting=true; document.getElementById('chatSendBtn').disabled=true;
    input.value=''; input.style.height='auto';
    addBubble('user',message); showTyping();
    try {
        const res=await fetch('/chat',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':`Bearer ${token}`},body:JSON.stringify({message,history:chatHistory})});
        document.getElementById('typingIndicator')?.remove();
        if(!res.ok){const e=await res.json();addBubble('ai',`Eroare: ${e.detail}`);return;}
        const data=await res.json();
        addBubble('ai',data.reply);
        chatHistory.push({role:'user',content:message}); chatHistory.push({role:'assistant',content:data.reply});
        if(chatHistory.length>20) chatHistory=chatHistory.slice(-20);
    } catch{document.getElementById('typingIndicator')?.remove();addBubble('ai','Eroare de conexiune. Verifică că serverul rulează.');}
    finally{chatWaiting=false;document.getElementById('chatSendBtn').disabled=false;input.focus();}
}

// ── STRIPE REDIRECT HANDLER ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Rulează la încărcarea paginii dacă vine de la checkout Stripe
(function handleStripeRedirect() {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const result = params.get('stripe');
    if (!result) return;

    // Curăță ?stripe=... din URL fără reload
    window.history.replaceState({}, '', '/');

    if (result === 'success') {
        // Webhook-ul Stripe procesează async — așteptăm 2s înainte să reîncărcăm
        const check = async () => {
            await loadPremiumStatus();
            if (_isPremium) {
                showToast('✦ Premium activat! Acum ai acces la toate funcțiile.');
                // Navighează la Settings să vadă statusul premium
                if (localStorage.getItem('coaching_token')) {
                    switchTab('settings');
                }
            } else {
                // Webhook poate întârzia — mai încearcă o dată după 3s
                setTimeout(async () => {
                    await loadPremiumStatus();
                    showToast(_isPremium
                        ? '✦ Premium activat! Acum ai acces la toate funcțiile.'
                        : 'Plata procesată — activarea poate dura câteva secunde.');
                }, 3000);
            }
        };
        setTimeout(check, 1800);

    } else if (result === 'cancel') {
        setTimeout(() => {
            showToast('Plata a fost anulată — planul gratuit rămâne activ.');
        }, 400);
    }
})();

        loadTemplates();
    }
    setTimeout(() => {
        if (localStorage.getItem('coaching_token')) loadStreak();
    }, 350);
});
