let _analyticsChart = null;

function _maybeShowPremiumNudge() {
    if (_isPremium) return;

    const key       = 'nl_nudge_dismissed';
    const dismissed = localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (dismissed && Date.now() - parseInt(dismissed) < 7 * 86400000) return; // o dată / 7 zile

    const existing = document.getElementById('premiumNudgeCard');
    if (existing) return;

    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.id    = 'premiumNudgeCard';
    el.style.cssText = 'margin-bottom:16px;animation:tabFadeIn .4s var(--ease)';
    el.innerHTML = `
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,rgba(196,98,45,0.08),transparent);
                    border:1px solid rgba(196,98,45,0.25);border-radius:var(--radius);
                    padding:22px 24px;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:16px">
            <div style="font-size:24px;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px">✦</div>
            <div style="flex:1">
                <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--accent);margin-bottom:5px">
                    Treci la Premium · 4.99€/lună
                </div>
                <div style="font-size:13px;color:var(--text-secondary);line-height:1.65;margin-bottom:14px">
                    Voice logging, plan AI 7 zile, rapoarte PDF, motor adaptiv și
                    analytics 4 săptămâni. Anulezi oricând.
                </div>
                <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">
                    <button onclick="startStripeCheckout()"
                            style="background:var(--accent);color:#fff;border:none;
                                   border-radius:var(--radius-sm);padding:10px 20px;
                                   font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;
                                   cursor:pointer;transition:opacity .15s;"
                            onmouseover="this.style.opacity='.85'"
                            onmouseout="this.style.opacity='1'">
                        Activează Premium
                    </button>
                    <button onclick="
                        localStorage.setItem('nl_nudge_dismissed', Date.now());
                        document.getElementById('premiumNudgeCard').remove();"
                            style="background:none;border:none;color:var(--text-muted);
                                   font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:12px;
                                   cursor:pointer;text-decoration:underline;padding:0;">
                        Acum nu
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>`;

    const analyticsTab = document.getElementById('tabAnalytics');
    if (analyticsTab) analyticsTab.insertBefore(el, analyticsTab.firstChild);
}

function _renderValueSummary() {
    const existingCard = document.getElementById('valueSummaryCard');
    if (existingCard) existingCard.remove();

    const foodCount    = _achCount('nl_food_count');
    const checkinCount = _achCount('nl_checkin_count');
    const streakMax    = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('nl_streak_max') || '0');

    // Agentul economisit estimat: nutriționist personal = ~150€/lună
    // 30 de zile de folosire = 150€ economisiți față de alternativă
    const daysActive   = Math.max(checkinCount, Math.round(foodCount / 3));
    const savedEst     = Math.round(daysActive * 5);  // ~5€/zi față de nutriționist

    if (foodCount < 3 && checkinCount < 1) return;  // prea puțin date

    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.id = 'valueSummaryCard';
    el.className = 'card';
    el.style.marginBottom = '16px';
    el.innerHTML = `
        <p class="card-label">Progresul Tău · Rezumat</p>
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:10px;margin-bottom:16px">
            <div style="background:var(--surface-2);border:1px solid var(--border);
                        border-radius:var(--radius-sm);padding:14px 12px;text-align:center">
                <div style="font-size:24px;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent);
                            letter-spacing:-0.03em;line-height:1;margin-bottom:5px" id="vsFood">
                    ${foodCount}
                </div>
                <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.08em;
                            text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--text-muted)">Mese loggate</div>
            </div>
            <div style="background:var(--surface-2);border:1px solid var(--border);
                        border-radius:var(--radius-sm);padding:14px 12px;text-align:center">
                <div style="font-size:24px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text-primary);
                            letter-spacing:-0.03em;line-height:1;margin-bottom:5px">
                    ${checkinCount}
                </div>
                <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.08em;
                            text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--text-muted)">Check-in-uri</div>
            </div>
            <div style="background:var(--surface-2);border:1px solid var(--border);
                        border-radius:var(--radius-sm);padding:14px 12px;text-align:center">
                <div style="font-size:24px;font-weight:700;color:var(--green);
                            letter-spacing:-0.03em;line-height:1;margin-bottom:5px">
                    ${savedEst > 0 ? savedEst + '€' : '—'}
                </div>
                <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.08em;
                            text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--text-muted)">
                    Economisit vs nutriționist
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- Achievements deblocate -->
        ${_renderAchievementsMini()}

        <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-muted);margin-top:12px;line-height:1.5;text-align:center">
            Estimare comparativă față de un nutriționist personal (~150€/lună).
        </p>`;

    const tab = document.getElementById('tabAnalytics');
    if (tab) tab.appendChild(el);
}

function _renderAchievementsMini() {
    const unlocked = _achGet();
    if (unlocked.length === 0) return '';

    const chips = unlocked.map(id => {
        const ach = _ACHIEVEMENTS.find(a => a.id === id);
        return ach
            ? `<span style="display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;
                            background:var(--surface-2);border:1px solid var(--border);
                            border-radius:999px;padding:4px 10px;font-size:12px;
                            color:var(--text-secondary)">
                    ${ach.icon} ${ach.title}
               </span>`
            : '';
    }).join('');

    return `
        <div style="padding-top:14px;border-top:1px solid var(--border)">
            <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:0.12em;
                        text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--text-muted);margin-bottom:10px">
                Achievements deblocate (${unlocked.length}/${_ACHIEVEMENTS.length})
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px">${chips}</div>
        </div>`;
}

async function loadAnalytics() {
    // Nudge Premium — apare discret după 3 zile de utilizare activă
    _maybeShowPremiumNudge();
    // Value Summary Card
    _renderValueSummary();
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    const lbl = document.getElementById('analyticsWeekLabel');
    if (lbl) lbl.textContent = 'Se încarcă...';
    try {
        // ── Weekly stats (FREE) ────────────────────────────────────────
        const weekRes = await fetch('/analytics/weekly', {
            headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token }
        });
        if (!weekRes.ok) {
            if (lbl) lbl.textContent = 'Eroare la încărcare.';
            return;
        }
        const weekData = await weekRes.json();
        _renderAnalyticsWeekCard(weekData);
        _renderAnalyticsDeltaCard(weekData.delta);

        // ── Trend 4 săptămâni (PREMIUM) ───────────────────────────────
        const trendsRes = await fetch('/analytics/trends', {
            headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token }
        });

        if (trendsRes.status === 402) {
            // Premium upsell — nu e eroare, e un feature gated
            const trendWrap  = document.getElementById('analyticsTrendWrap');
            const trendEmpty = document.getElementById('analyticsTrendEmpty');
            const trendLabel = document.getElementById('analyticsTrendLabel');
            if (trendLabel) trendLabel.textContent = 'Grafic Trend · 4 Săptămâni';
            if (trendWrap)  trendWrap.style.display = 'none';
            if (trendEmpty) {
                trendEmpty.style.display = 'block';
                trendEmpty.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="background:var(--accent-glow);border:1px solid rgba(196,98,45,0.3);
                                border-radius:12px;padding:22px 18px;text-align:center;">
                        <div style="font-size:22px;margin-bottom:10px">✦</div>
                        <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text-primary);margin-bottom:6px">
                            Grafic Trend · Premium
                        </div>
                        <div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text-secondary);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:16px">
                            Mediile zilnice ale ultimelor 4 săptămâni<br>
                            comparate cu targetul tău caloric.
                        </div>
                        <button onclick="startStripeCheckout()"
                                style="background:var(--accent);color:#fff;border:none;
                                       border-radius:8px;padding:10px 22px;font-size:13px;
                                       font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;letter-spacing:0.02em;">
                            ✦ Activează Premium
                        </button>
                    </div>`;
            }
            return;
        }
if (!trendsRes.ok) return;
        const trendsData = await trendsRes.json();
        _renderAnalyticsTrendsChart(trendsData);

    } catch(e) { console.warn('loadAnalytics:', e); }
}

// ── PREMIUM STATUS (global scope) ────────────────────────────────────────────
let _premiumData = null;

async function loadPremiumStatus() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) {
        _showPremiumState('free');
        return;
    }
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/stripe/status', {
            headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token }
        });
        if (res.ok) {
            const data   = await res.json();
            _isPremium   = Boolean(data.is_premium);
            _premiumData = data;
            _updatePremiumUI();
        } else {
            _showPremiumState('free');
        }
    } catch(e) {
        console.warn('loadPremiumStatus:', e);
        _showPremiumState('free');
    }
}

function _showPremiumState(state) {
    const loading = document.getElementById('premiumLoadingState');
    const free    = document.getElementById('premiumFreeState');
    const active  = document.getElementById('premiumActiveState');
    if (loading) loading.style.display = state === 'loading' ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (free)    free.style.display    = state === 'free'    ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (active)  active.style.display  = state === 'active'  ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function _updatePremiumUI() {
    if (_isPremium) {
        _showPremiumState('active');
        const expiryEl = document.getElementById('premiumExpiryText');
        if (expiryEl) {
            const until = _premiumData?.premium_until;
            if (until) {
                const d = new Date(until);
                const MO = ['ian','feb','mar','apr','mai','iun',
                            'iul','aug','sep','oct','nov','dec'];
                expiryEl.textContent =
                    `Activ până la ${d.getDate()} ${MO[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getFullYear()}`;
            } else {
                expiryEl.textContent = 'Abonament activ';
            }
        }
    } else {
        _showPremiumState('free');
    }

    const badge = document.getElementById('premiumBadgeStreak');
    if (badge) badge.style.display = _isPremium ? 'inline-block' : 'none';

    const btnV = document.getElementById('btnVoice');
    const btnB = document.getElementById('btnBarcode');
    if (btnV) btnV.title = _isPremium
        ? 'Logare prin voce — click pentru a înregistra'
        : '🔒 Premium — Logare prin voce';
    if (btnB) btnB.title = _isPremium
        ? 'Scanează cod de bare EAN/UPC — macros din Open Food Facts'
        : '🔒 Premium — Scanează cod de bare';
}

function showPremiumUpsell(feature) {
    showToast(`🔒 ${feature} · doar Premium — apasă ✦ pentru a activa`, true);
    setTimeout(() => startStripeCheckout(), 1800);
}

function _renderAnalyticsWeekCard(data) {
    const w = data.current_week || {};
    const avg = w.avg_daily || {};
    const comp = data.compliance || {};
    const lbl = document.getElementById('analyticsWeekLabel');
    if (lbl && w.week_start) {
        const MO = ['Ian','Feb','Mar','Apr','Mai','Iun','Iul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
        const ws = new Date(w.week_start+'T12:00:00'), we = new Date(w.week_end+'T12:00:00');
        lbl.textContent = ws.getDate()+' '+MO[ws.getMonth()]+' – '+we.getDate()+' '+MO[we.getMonth()];
    }
    const empty = document.getElementById('analyticsEmptyState');
    const grid  = document.getElementById('analyticsStatGrid');
    const daysRow = document.getElementById('analyticsDaysRow');
    const compRow = document.getElementById('analyticsComplianceRow');
    const compBadge = document.getElementById('analyticsComplianceBadge');
    if (!w.days_logged) {
        if (grid)    grid.style.display    = 'none';
        if (daysRow) daysRow.style.display = 'none';
        if (compRow) compRow.style.display = 'none';
        if (empty) {
            empty.style.display = 'block';
            empty.innerHTML = `
                <div class="empty-state" style="padding:32px 0">
                    <div class="empty-state-icon">📊</div>
                    <div class="empty-state-title">Nicio dată această săptămână</div>
                    <div class="empty-state-sub">
                        Loghează mese din tabul Nutriție. Analytics apar
                        automat după prima zi cu date.
                    </div>
                </div>`;
        }
        _renderAnalyticsWeekDots(w.daily_breakdown);
        return;
    }
    if (empty) empty.style.display='none'; if (grid) grid.style.display='grid';
    if (daysRow) { daysRow.style.display='block'; const de=document.getElementById('analyticsDaysLogged'); if(de) de.textContent=w.days_logged; }
    _setAnalyticsStat('aCalories','aCaloriesBar',avg.calories,comp.calories_pct);
    _setAnalyticsStat('aProtein','aProteinBar',avg.protein_g,comp.protein_pct);
    _setAnalyticsStat('aCarbs','aCarbsBar',avg.carbs_g,comp.carbs_pct);
    _setAnalyticsStat('aFat','aFatBar',avg.fat_g,comp.fat_pct);
    if (compRow && compBadge && Object.keys(comp).length) {
        const vals=[comp.calories_pct,comp.protein_pct,comp.carbs_pct,comp.fat_pct].filter(v=>v>0);
        compBadge.textContent=Math.round(vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/vals.length)+'%';
        compRow.style.display='flex';
    }
    _renderAnalyticsWeekDots(w.daily_breakdown);
}
function _setAnalyticsStat(vId,bId,val,pct) {
    const v=document.getElementById(vId), b=document.getElementById(bId);
    if(v) v.textContent=val??'—';
    if(b&&pct!==undefined){b.style.width=Math.min(100,pct)+'%';b.style.opacity=(pct>=90&&pct<=115)?'1':pct>=75?'0.75':'0.45';}
}
function _renderAnalyticsWeekDots(daily) {
    const c=document.getElementById('analyticsWeekDots'); if(!c||!Array.isArray(daily)) return;
    c.innerHTML=daily.map(d=>{
        const cls=['analytics-week-dot',d.has_logs?'has-log':'',d.is_future?'is-future':''].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
        return `<div class="${cls}" title="${d.date}"><span class="analytics-week-dot-day">${d.weekday}</span>${d.has_logs?`<span class="analytics-week-dot-kcal">${d.calories}</span>`:''}</div>`;
    }).join('');
}
function _renderAnalyticsTrendsChart(data) {
    const weeks=data.weeks||[], wrap=document.getElementById('analyticsTrendWrap'), empty=document.getElementById('analyticsTrendEmpty'), lbl=document.getElementById('analyticsTrendLabel');
    if(weeks.length<2){if(wrap)wrap.style.display='none';if(empty)empty.style.display='block';return;}
    if(wrap)wrap.style.display='block';if(empty)empty.style.display='none';
    const target=data.target_kcal;
    if(lbl&&target) lbl.textContent=`Medie zilnică vs target (${target} kcal)`;
    const r=document.documentElement, accent=getComputedStyle(r).getPropertyValue('--accent').trim()||'#c4622d', pClr=getComputedStyle(r).getPropertyValue('--protein').trim()||'#e07b45';
    const datasets=[
        {label:'Calorii medii',data:weeks.map(w=>w.calories),borderColor:accent,backgroundColor:accent+'18',fill:true,tension:0.4,borderWidth:2,pointRadius:5,yAxisID:'y'},
        {label:'Proteină (g)',data:weeks.map(w=>w.protein_g),borderColor:pClr,backgroundColor:'transparent',fill:false,tension:0.4,borderWidth:1.5,borderDash:[4,3],pointRadius:3,yAxisID:'y2'},
    ];
    if(target) datasets.push({label:'Target',data:new Array(weeks.length).fill(target),borderColor:'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)',backgroundColor:'transparent',borderDash:[6,4],borderWidth:1,pointRadius:0,yAxisID:'y'});
    const ctx=document.getElementById('analyticsChart'); if(!ctx) return;
    if(_analyticsChart){_analyticsChart.destroy();_analyticsChart=null;}
    _analyticsChart=new Chart(ctx,{type:'line',data:{labels:weeks.map(w=>w.week_label),datasets},options:{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,interaction:{mode:'index',intersect:false},plugins:{legend:{labels:{color:'#8e8880',font:{size:11,family:'Inter'},boxWidth:16,padding:12}},tooltip:{backgroundColor:'#161616',borderColor:'#2e2e2e',borderWidth:1,titleColor:'#ede8df',bodyColor:'#8e8880',padding:10,callbacks:{label:c=>` ${c.dataset.label}: ${c.raw}${c.datasetIndex===1?' g':' kcal'}`}}},scales:{x:{ticks:{color:'#8e8880',font:{size:11}},grid:{color:'#1e1e1e'}},y:{position:'left',ticks:{color:'#8e8880',font:{size:11}},grid:{color:'#1e1e1e'}},y2:{position:'right',ticks:{color:pClr+'aa',font:{size:11}},grid:{display:false}}}}});
}
function _renderAnalyticsDeltaCard(delta) {
    const card=document.getElementById('analyticsDeltaCard'), grid=document.getElementById('analyticsDeltaGrid');
    if(!card||!grid||!delta){if(card)card.style.display='none';return;}
    card.style.display='block';
    const items=[{label:'Calorii',key:'calories',unit:'kcal'},{label:'Proteină',key:'protein_g',unit:'g'},{label:'Carbohidrați',key:'carbs_g',unit:'g'},{label:'Grăsimi',key:'fat_g',unit:'g'}];
    grid.innerHTML=items.map(item=>{const v=Math.round(delta[item.key]||0),cls=v>10?'pos':v<-10?'neg':'neutral',pfx=v>0?'+':'';return`<div class="analytics-delta-item"><span class="analytics-delta-lbl">${item.label}</span><span class="analytics-delta-val ${cls}">${pfx}${v} <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:400;opacity:.7">${item.unit}</span></span></div>`;}).join('');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  E8 · STREAK ENGINE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function loadStreak() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/streak', { headers:{ 'Authorization':'Bearer '+token } });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        _renderStreak(await res.json());
    } catch(e) {}
}

function _renderStreak(data) {
    const bar    = document.getElementById('streakBar');
    const count  = document.getElementById('streakCount');
    const sub    = document.getElementById('streakSub');
    const dots   = document.getElementById('streakDots');
    const record = document.getElementById('streakRecord');
    if (!bar) return;

    const streak = data.current_streak || 0;
    const alive  = data.is_alive && streak > 0;
    bar.classList.toggle('alive', alive);

    if (count) count.textContent = streak;

    if (sub) {
        if (!streak) {
            sub.textContent = 'Nicio zi logată. Începe azi!';
        } else if (alive && !data.logged_today) {
            sub.textContent = (streak===1?'zi consecutivă':'zile consecutive') + ' · Loghează azi!';
        } else if (alive && data.logged_today) {
            sub.textContent = (streak===1?'zi consecutivă':'zile consecutive') + ' · Azi ✓';
        } else {
            sub.textContent = 'Streak rupt · Repornești de la 0';
        }
    }

    if (dots && data.last_7_days) {
        dots.innerHTML = data.last_7_days.map(d => {
            const cls = ['streak-dot', d.logged?'logged':'', d.is_today?'today':''].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
            return `<div class="${cls}" title="${d.weekday}"></div>`;
        }).join('');
    }

    if (record) {
        const parts = [];
        if (data.longest_streak > 1) parts.push(`Maxim: ${data.longest_streak}`);
        record.textContent = parts.join(' · ');
    }
    // Achievement check streak
    if ((data.current_streak || 0) > 0) {
        _checkAchievements('streak', data.current_streak);
    }
    // Actualizează streak bar culoare bazat pe starea de azi
    const flame = document.getElementById('streakFlame');
    if (flame) {
        flame.style.filter = (data.is_alive && data.logged_today)
            ? 'none'
            : data.is_alive
                ? 'saturate(0.5) brightness(0.8)'
                : 'grayscale(1) opacity(.35)';
    }
}

// ── SETTINGS ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ETAPA 5 · SETTINGS HUB ENGINE
//  Accent Color · Density · System Sync · loadSettings · saveSettings
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ── Accent Presets ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const ACCENT_PRESETS = {
    amber:   { main:'#c4622d', hover:'#d4722d', glow:'rgba(196,98,45,0.08)',  glow2:'rgba(196,98,45,0.18)',  label:'Neon Amber'    },
    cyan:    { main:'#00b4d8', hover:'#00c8f0', glow:'rgba(0,180,216,0.08)',  glow2:'rgba(0,180,216,0.18)',  label:'Electric Cyan' },
    emerald: { main:'#22c55e', hover:'#16a34a', glow:'rgba(34,197,94,0.08)',  glow2:'rgba(34,197,94,0.18)',  label:'Emerald Green' },
    violet:  { main:'#8b5cf6', hover:'#7c3aed', glow:'rgba(139,92,246,0.08)', glow2:'rgba(139,92,246,0.18)', label:'Cyber Violet'  },
    white:   { main:'#d4d0cb', hover:'#e4e0db', glow:'rgba(212,208,203,0.06)',glow2:'rgba(212,208,203,0.14)',label:'Mono White'    },
};

function applyAccent(key) {
    const p = ACCENT_PRESETS[key] || ACCENT_PRESETS.amber;
    const r = document.documentElement;
    r.style.setProperty('--accent',       p.main);
    r.style.setProperty('--accent-hover', p.hover);
    r.style.setProperty('--accent-glow',  p.glow);
    r.style.setProperty('--accent-glow-2',p.glow2);
    // Persistă local — zero-flicker script îl preia la refresh
    localStorage.setItem('ce_accent', key);
    // Actualizează UI swatches
    document.querySelectorAll('.accent-swatch').forEach(sw => {
        sw.classList.toggle('active', sw.dataset.accent === key);
    });
    const lbl = document.getElementById('accentCurrentLabel');
    if (lbl) lbl.textContent = '● ' + p.label;
}

function selectAccent(key) {
    applyAccent(key);
    // Auto-save imediat la backend (fără buton explicit)
    _autoSaveSettingsDebounced();
}

// ── Density ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function applyDensity(key) {
    const valid = ['compact','comfortable','spacious'];
    if (!valid.includes(key)) key = 'comfortable';
    document.documentElement.dataset.density = key;
    localStorage.setItem('ce_density', key);
    // Actualizează UI toggle
    document.querySelectorAll('.density-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.dataset.density === key);
    });
}

function selectDensity(key) {
    applyDensity(key);
    _autoSaveSettingsDebounced();
}

// ── Theme ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function applyTheme(theme) {
    const r = document.documentElement;
    if (theme === 'amoled') {
        r.style.setProperty('--bg',       '#000000');
        r.style.setProperty('--surface',  '#0a0a0a');
        r.style.setProperty('--surface-2','#0f0f0f');
    } else {
        r.style.setProperty('--bg',       '#080808');
        r.style.setProperty('--surface',  '#111111');
        r.style.setProperty('--surface-2','#161616');
    }
    localStorage.setItem('ce_theme', theme);
}

// ── System Sync ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function updateSyncUI(isAuto) {
    const dot  = document.getElementById('syncDot');
    const txt  = document.getElementById('syncStatusText');
    const opts = document.getElementById('themeOptions');
    if (!dot || !txt || !opts) return;

    if (isAuto) {
        const prefersDark = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
        dot.classList.add('active');
        txt.textContent = `Auto — sistemul tău e ${prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light'} · aplicând Dark`;
        opts.classList.add('disabled');
        applyTheme('dark');   // app dark-first — light OS → rămâne dark
    } else {
        dot.classList.remove('active');
        txt.textContent = 'Manual — tu controlezi tema';
        opts.classList.remove('disabled');
    }
}

function onThemeSyncChange() {
    const isAuto = document.getElementById('toggleThemeSync')?.checked || false;
    localStorage.setItem('ce_theme_sync', isAuto ? 'auto' : 'manual');
    updateSyncUI(isAuto);
    _autoSaveSettingsDebounced();
}

// Listener OS preference change (când userul schimbă dark/light mode pe telefon)
window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').addEventListener('change', () => {
    const isAuto = document.getElementById('toggleThemeSync')?.checked || false;
    if (isAuto) updateSyncUI(true);
});

// ── Reduce Animations ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function applyReduceAnimations(reduce) {
    if (reduce) {
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--ease', 'linear');
        document.documentElement.classList.add('reduce-motion');
    } else {
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--ease', 'cubic-bezier(0.4,0,0.2,1)');
        document.documentElement.classList.remove('reduce-motion');
    }
}

// ── Auto-save debounce ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Accent, density, sync se auto-salvează 600ms după ultima schimbare
let _autoSaveTimer = null;
function _autoSaveSettingsDebounced() {
    clearTimeout(_autoSaveTimer);
    _autoSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        saveSettings(true); // silent=true → nu afișa feedback
    }, 600);
}

// ── loadSettings ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function loadSettings() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/settings', { headers:{'Authorization':'Bearer '+token} });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const s = await res.json();

        // Temă
        const thEl = document.getElementById('theme-' + (s.theme||'dark'));
        if (thEl) thEl.checked = true;
        applyTheme(s.theme || 'dark');

        // [E5] Accent
        applyAccent(s.accent_color || 'amber');

        // [E5] Density
        applyDensity(s.density || 'comfortable');

        // [E5] System Sync
        const syncEl = document.getElementById('toggleThemeSync');
        const isAuto = (s.theme_sync === 'auto');
        if (syncEl) syncEl.checked = isAuto;
        updateSyncUI(isAuto);

        // Persona
        const peEl = document.getElementById('persona-' + (s.ai_persona||'empatic'));
        if (peEl) peEl.checked = true;

        // Diet template
        const dtMap = {standard:'standard',keto:'keto',mediteranean:'med',if_16_8:'if'};
        const dtEl = document.getElementById('dt-' + (dtMap[s.diet_template]||'standard'));
        if (dtEl) dtEl.checked = true;

        // Aggressiveness
        const agEl = document.getElementById('agg-' + (s.adaptive_aggressiveness||2));
        if (agEl) agEl.checked = true;

        // Allergies
        const allEl = document.getElementById('settingsAllergies');
        if (allEl) allEl.value = s.allergies || '';

        // Reduce animations
        const animEl = document.getElementById('toggleReduceAnim');
        if (animEl) {
            animEl.checked = s.reduce_animations || false;
            applyReduceAnimations(animEl.checked);
        }

    } catch(e) { console.warn('loadSettings error:', e); }
}

// ── saveSettings ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function saveSettings(silent = false) {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const btn = document.getElementById('settingsSaveBtn');
    const fb  = document.getElementById('settingsFeedback');

    if (!silent) {
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.textContent = 'Se salvează...';
    }

    const payload = {
        theme:                   document.querySelector('input[name="theme"]:checked')?.value || 'dark',
        accent_color:            localStorage.getItem('ce_accent') || 'amber',              // [E5]
        density:                 localStorage.getItem('ce_density') || 'comfortable',       // [E5]
        theme_sync:              document.getElementById('toggleThemeSync')?.checked         // [E5]
                                     ? 'auto' : 'manual',
        ai_persona:              document.querySelector('input[name="persona"]:checked')?.value || 'empatic',
        diet_template:           document.querySelector('input[name="diet_template"]:checked')?.value || 'standard',
        allergies:               document.getElementById('settingsAllergies')?.value.trim() || '',
        adaptive_aggressiveness: parseInt(document.querySelector('input[name="aggressiveness"]:checked')?.value || '2'),
        reduce_animations:       document.getElementById('toggleReduceAnim')?.checked || false,
        units:                   'metric',
    };

    try {
        const res = await fetch('/settings', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+token},
            body: JSON.stringify(payload),
        });
        if (res.ok) {
            applyTheme(payload.theme);
            if (!silent) {
                fb.style.color = 'var(--green)';
                fb.textContent = '✓ Settings salvate';
                setTimeout(() => { fb.textContent = ''; }, 3000);
            }
        } else {
            if (!silent) { fb.style.color = '#ef4444'; fb.textContent = 'Eroare la salvare.'; }
        }
    } catch(e) {
        if (!silent) { fb.style.color = '#ef4444'; fb.textContent = 'Eroare de rețea.'; }
    }

    if (!silent) {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = 'Salvează Settings';
    }
}
async function exportData(format) {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/settings/export/'+format, { headers:{'Authorization':'Bearer '+token} });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const blob = await res.blob();
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url; a.download = 'coaching_export.'+format; a.click();
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch(e) { alert('Eroare la export.'); }
}
function showDeleteConfirm() {
    document.getElementById('deleteConfirmRow').classList.add('visible');
    document.getElementById('btnDeleteStep1').style.display = 'none';
}
async function deleteAccount() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnConfirmDelete');
    btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Se șterge...';
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/settings/account', { method:'DELETE', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+token}, body:JSON.stringify({confirm:true}) });
        if (res.ok) { localStorage.removeItem('coaching_token'); alert('Contul a fost șters definitiv.'); window.location.reload(); }
        else { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Da, șterge definitiv'; }
    } catch(e) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Da, șterge definitiv'; }
}

// ── ACCOUNT SECTION ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async function loadAccountSection() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    // ── 1. Avatar + email ───────────────────────────────────────
    const email = getUserEmailFromToken();
    if (email) {
        document.getElementById('acct-email').textContent = email;
        const prefix   = email.split('@')[0];
        const initials = prefix.length >= 2
            ? (prefix[0] + prefix[1]).toUpperCase()
            : prefix[0].toUpperCase();
        var initialsEl = document.getElementById('acct-avatar-initials');
        if (initialsEl) initialsEl.textContent = initials;
        // Dacă există avatar custom, nu suprascrie inițialele vizual
        if (!localStorage.getItem('nlAvatarData')) {
          var imgEl = document.getElementById('acct-avatar-img');
          if (imgEl) imgEl.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }

    // ── 2. Status abonament ─────────────────────────────────────
    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/stripe/status', { headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } });
        const data = res.ok ? await res.json() : {};

        document.getElementById('acct-sub-loading').style.display = 'none';

        if (data.is_premium) {
            document.getElementById('acct-sub-premium').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('acct-premium-badge').style.display = 'block';
            if (data.premium_until) {
                const d   = new Date(data.premium_until);
                const lun = ['ian','feb','mar','apr','mai','iun',
                              'iul','aug','sep','oct','nov','dec'];
                document.getElementById('acct-until').textContent =
                    `Activ până la ${d.getDate()} ${lun[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getFullYear()}`;
            }
        } else {
            document.getElementById('acct-sub-free').style.display   = 'block';
            document.getElementById('acct-free-badge').style.display = 'block';
        }
    } catch(e) {
        document.getElementById('acct-sub-loading').textContent = 'Status indisponibil momentan.';
    }

    // ── 3. Date profil ──────────────────────────────────────────
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/profile/get', { headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } });
        if (res.ok) {
            const { profile: p } = await res.json();
            if (p) {
                if (p.height_cm)         document.getElementById('prof-height').value   = p.height_cm;
                if (p.age)               document.getElementById('prof-age').value      = p.age;
                if (p.initial_weight_kg) document.getElementById('prof-weight').value   = p.initial_weight_kg;
                if (p.goal)              document.getElementById('prof-goal').value      = p.goal;
                if (p.activity_level)    document.getElementById('prof-activity').value  = p.activity_level;
                if (p.sex) {
                    const r = document.getElementById(`prof-sex-${p.sex}`);
                    if (r) r.checked = true;
                }
            }
        }
    } catch(e) { /* profil indisponibil — câmpurile rămân goale */ }

    // ── 4. Admin link ───────────────────────────────────────────
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/auth/is-admin', { headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token } });
        if (res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data.is_admin) {
                document.getElementById('acct-admin-link').style.display = 'block';
            }
        }
    } catch(e) { /* tăcut */ }
}

async function saveProfileFromCont() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const height   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('prof-height').value);
    const age      = parseInt(document.getElementById('prof-age').value);
    const weight   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('prof-weight').value);
    const sex      = document.querySelector('input[name="prof-sex"]:checked')?.value || '';
    const goal     = document.getElementById('prof-goal').value;
    const activity = document.getElementById('prof-activity').value;

    if (!height || height < 100 || height > 250) { _profFb('⚠ Înălțime invalidă (100–250 cm)', false); return; }
    if (!age    || age < 14    || age > 99)       { _profFb('⚠ Vârstă invalidă (14–99 ani)',     false); return; }
    if (!weight || weight < 20 || weight > 300)   { _profFb('⚠ Greutate invalidă (20–300 kg)',   false); return; }
    if (!sex)                                      { _profFb('⚠ Selectează sexul',                false); return; }
    if (!goal)                                     { _profFb('⚠ Selectează obiectivul',            false); return; }
    if (!activity)                                 { _profFb('⚠ Selectează nivelul de activitate', false); return; }

    const btn  = document.getElementById('profSaveBtn');
    const txt  = document.getElementById('profSaveBtnText');
    btn.disabled    = true;
    txt.textContent = 'Se salvează...';

    try {
        const res = await fetch('/profile/save', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token },
            body:    JSON.stringify({
                height_cm:         height,
                age:               age,
                sex:               sex,
                activity_level:    activity,
                goal:              goal,
                initial_weight_kg: weight,
            }),
        });

        if (res.ok) {
            _profFb('✓ Profil salvat cu succes!', true);
            // Actualizează și prefill-ul din formularul principal
            loadProfileAndPrefill();
        } else {
            const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            _profFb('⚠ ' + (err.detail || 'Eroare la salvare'), false);
        }
    } catch(e) {
        _profFb('⚠ Eroare de conexiune', false);
    } finally {
        btn.disabled    = false;
        txt.textContent = 'Salvează Profilul';
    }
}

function _profFb(msg, ok) {
    const el = document.getElementById('profSaveFeedback');
    el.textContent = msg;
    el.style.color = ok ? 'var(--green)' : '#ef4444';
    if (ok) setTimeout(() => { el.textContent = ''; }, 3500);
}


function doLogout() {
    // Confirmăm cu userul
    if (!confirm('Ești sigur că vrei să te deconectezi?')) return;

    // Ștergem DOAR token-ul — preferințele (temă, accent, densitate) rămân
    localStorage.removeItem('coaching_token');

    // Ascundem navigarea și arătăm overlay-ul de login
    const nav = document.getElementById('sectionNav');
    if (nav) nav.style.display = 'none';
    showAuthOverlay();
    switchAuthTab('login');
    showAuthMsg('Ai fost deconectat.', 'success');
}

async function startCheckout() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/stripe/checkout', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (res.ok && data.checkout_url) {
            window.location.href = data.checkout_url;
        } else {
            showToast(data.detail || 'Nu s-a putut inițializa plata.', true);
        }
    } catch(e) {
        showToast('Eroare la inițializarea plății.', true);
    }
}

// ── ONBOARDING ───────────────────────────────────────────────

async function checkOnboarding() {
    // Dacă userul a închis manual onboarding-ul → nu mai apare
    if (localStorage.getItem('coaching_ob_done') === '1') return;

    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const h = { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` };
    try {
        const [r1, r2, r3] = await Promise.all([
            fetch('/auth/my-sessions', { headers: h }),
            fetch('/checkin/history',  { headers: h }),
            fetch('/food/today',       { headers: h }),
        ]);
        const [sess, ci, food] = await Promise.all([
            r1.json(), r2.json(), r3.json()
        ]);

        const s1 = sess.count > 0;
        const s2 = ci.count  > 0;
        const s3 = (food.summary?.logs_count || 0) > 0;

        // Bifează pașii deja completați
        if (s1) _obMarkDone(1);
        if (s2) _obMarkDone(2);
        if (s3) _obMarkDone(3);

        // Toți 3 gata → marchează permanent și nu mai afișa
        if (s1 && s2 && s3) {
            localStorage.setItem('coaching_ob_done', '1');
            return;
        }

        document.getElementById('onboardingOverlay').style.display = 'flex';
    } catch { /* silențios — onboarding e opțional */ }
}

function _obMarkDone(step) {
    document.getElementById(`obStep${step}`)?.classList.add('ob-done');
    const num = document.getElementById(`obNum${step}`);
    if (num) num.textContent = '✓';
}

function obStart() {
    // Navighează la primul pas necompletat
    const s1done = document.getElementById('obStep1')?.classList.contains('ob-done');
    const s2done = document.getElementById('obStep2')?.classList.contains('ob-done');
    obDismiss();
    if (!s1done)      switchTab('historic');
    else if (!s2done) switchTab('progres');
    else              switchTab('nutritie');
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
}

function obGo(tab) {
    obDismiss();
    switchTab(tab);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
}

function obDismiss() {
    document.getElementById('onboardingOverlay').style.display = 'none';
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  PUSH NOTIFICATIONS — Web Push API + VAPID
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

let _pushCurrentEndpoint = null;

function _urlBase64ToUint8Array(base64String) {
    const padding = '='.repeat((4 - base64String.length % 4) % 4);
    const base64  = (base64String + padding).replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    const raw     = atob(base64);
    const output  = new Uint8Array(raw.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) output[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i);
    return output;
}

function _setPushStatus(subscribed, message) {
    const dot     = document.getElementById('pushStatusDot');
    const text    = document.getElementById('pushStatusText');
    const toggle  = document.getElementById('togglePushNotif');
    const title   = document.getElementById('pushToggleTitle');
    const sub     = document.getElementById('pushToggleSub');
    const testRow = document.getElementById('pushTestRow');
    if (!dot) return;
    dot.style.background = subscribed ? 'var(--green)' : 'var(--text-muted)';
    text.textContent     = message;
    toggle.checked       = subscribed;
    if (subscribed) {
        title.textContent     = 'Notificările sunt Active';
        sub.textContent       = 'Vei fi reamintit la 20:00 dacă nu ai logat azi.';
        testRow.style.display = 'block';
    } else {
        title.textContent     = 'Activează Notificările';
        sub.textContent       = 'Reminder zilnic la 20:00 dacă nu ai logat azi. Funcționează și cu ecranul blocat.';
        testRow.style.display = 'none';
    }
}

function _setPushError(msg) {
    const errDiv = document.getElementById('pushBrowserError');
    if (!errDiv) return;
    errDiv.textContent   = msg;
    errDiv.style.display = msg ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function _pushSupported() {
    if (!('serviceWorker' in navigator)) return 'Service Worker nesuportat de browser.';
    if (!('PushManager' in window))      return 'Web Push nesuportat de browser. Încearcă Chrome, Edge sau Firefox.';
    if (!('Notification' in window))     return 'Notificările nu sunt suportate de browser.';
    return null;
}

async function initPushNotifications() {
    const card = document.getElementById('pushNotifCard');
    if (!card) return;
    const unsupported = _pushSupported();
    if (unsupported) {
        _setPushStatus(false, unsupported);
        card.style.opacity = '0.5';
        return;
    }
    if (Notification.permission === 'denied') {
        _setPushStatus(false, 'Notificările sunt blocate în browser. Deblochează-le din setările site-ului.');
        _setPushError('Mergi la setările browserului → Privacy → Notificații → permite pentru acest site.');
        return;
    }
    try {
        const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
        if (!token) return;
        const res  = await fetch('/push/status', { headers:{ 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` } });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.subscribed && data.count > 0) {
            const reg       = await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
            const browserSub = await reg.pushManager.getSubscription();
            if (browserSub) {
                _pushCurrentEndpoint = browserSub.endpoint;
                _setPushStatus(true, `Activ · ${data.count} dispozitiv${data.count > 1 ? 'e' : ''}`);
            } else {
                _setPushStatus(false, 'Dezactivat (subscription expirat)');
            }
        } else {
            _setPushStatus(false, 'Dezactivat · Click pe toggle pentru a activa');
        }
    } catch { _setPushStatus(false, 'Status indisponibil'); }
}

async function onPushToggleChange(checked) {
    _setPushError('');
    if (checked) { await _subscribeToPush(); }
    else         { await _unsubscribeFromPush(); }
}

async function _subscribeToPush() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    let vapidPublicKey;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/push/vapid-key');
        if (!res.ok) { _setPushStatus(false, 'Notificările nu sunt disponibile momentan.'); return; }
        vapidPublicKey = (await res.json()).vapid_public_key;
    } catch { _setPushStatus(false, 'Eroare de conexiune.'); return; }

    let permission;
    try { permission = await Notification.requestPermission(); }
    catch { permission = 'denied'; }
    if (permission !== 'granted') {
        _setPushStatus(false, 'Permisiunea pentru notificări a fost refuzată.');
        if (permission === 'denied') _setPushError('Notificările sunt blocate. Mergi la setările browserului → permite pentru acest site.');
        return;
    }

    let subscription;
    try {
        const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
        subscription = await reg.pushManager.subscribe({
            userVisibleOnly:      true,
            applicationServerKey: _urlBase64ToUint8Array(vapidPublicKey),
        });
    } catch (err) {
        _setPushStatus(false, 'Nu s-a putut crea subscription-ul push.');
        _setPushError(`Eroare: ${err.message}`);
        return;
    }

    try {
        const subJson = subscription.toJSON();
        const res = await fetch('/push/subscribe', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` },
            body:    JSON.stringify({ endpoint:subJson.endpoint, p256dh:subJson.keys.p256dh, auth:subJson.keys.auth }),
        });
        if (!res.ok) { _setPushStatus(false, 'Eroare la salvarea subscription-ului.'); return; }
        _pushCurrentEndpoint = subJson.endpoint;
        _setPushStatus(true, 'Activ · Vei primi reminder zilnic la 20:00');
    } catch { _setPushStatus(false, 'Eroare de rețea.'); }
}

async function _unsubscribeFromPush() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const reg = await navigator.serviceWorker.ready;
        const sub = await reg.pushManager.getSubscription();
        if (sub) await sub.unsubscribe();
    } catch { /* continuăm */ }
    if (_pushCurrentEndpoint) {
        try {
            await fetch('/push/unsubscribe', {
                method:  'DELETE',
                headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` },
                body:    JSON.stringify({ endpoint: _pushCurrentEndpoint }),
            });
        } catch { /* nu blocăm UI */ }
        _pushCurrentEndpoint = null;
    }
    _setPushStatus(false, 'Dezactivat · Notificările sunt oprite.');
}

// ── P7: Download Raport Săptămânal PDF ───────────────────────────────────
async function downloadWeeklyReport(weekOffset = 0) {
  const btn = document.getElementById('btnDownloadReport');
  const orig = btn ? btn.textContent : '';
  if (btn) { btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Se generează... (~5s)'; }

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/report/weekly?week_offset=${weekOffset}`, {
      headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('coaching_token')}` },
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      showToast(err.detail || 'Eroare la generare raport', true);
      return;
    }

    const blob = await res.blob();
    const url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a    = document.createElement('a');
    a.href     = url;
    a.download = `raport_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.pdf`;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    showToast('✓ Raport descărcat!');
  } catch (e) {
    showToast('⚠ Eroare conexiune', true);
  } finally {
    if (btn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = orig; }
  }
}

async function sendPushTest() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res  = await fetch('/push/test', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Content-Type':'application/json', 'Authorization':`Bearer ${token}` },
            body:    JSON.stringify({ endpoint: _pushCurrentEndpoint }),
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) {
            _setPushError(data.detail || 'Eroare la trimiterea testului.');
        } else {
            const btn  = event.currentTarget;
            const orig = btn.textContent;
            btn.textContent  = '✅ Trimis!';
            btn.style.color  = 'var(--green)';
            setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = orig; btn.style.color = ''; }, 2000);
        }
    } catch { _setPushError('Eroare de rețea la trimiterea testului.'); }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// #18 · QUICK MEAL TEMPLATES
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
let _pendingTemplate = null;
let _suggestAutoHide = null;

async function loadTemplates() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('/food/templates', {
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
        });
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        _renderTemplatesStrip(data.templates || []);
    } catch { /* best-effort */ }
}

function _renderTemplatesStrip(templates) {
    const wrapper = document.getElementById('templatesStripWrapper');
    const strip   = document.getElementById('templatesStrip');
    const counter = document.getElementById('templatesCount');
    if (!wrapper || !strip) return;

    if (!templates || templates.length === 0) {
        wrapper.style.display = 'none';
        return;
    }

    if (counter) counter.textContent = `(${templates.length})`;

    strip.innerHTML = templates.map(t => {
        const name = escapeHtml(t.name.length > 28 ? t.name.slice(0, 28) + '…' : t.name);
        return `
        <button class="template-chip" id="tmpl-${t.id}"
                onclick="useTemplate(${t.id})"
                title="${escapeHtml(t.description)} · ${t.calories} kcal">
            <span class="template-chip-inner">
                <span class="template-chip-name">${name}</span>
                <span class="template-chip-cal">${t.calories} kcal</span>
            </span>
            <button class="template-chip-del"
                onclick="event.stopPropagation(); deleteTemplate(${t.id})"
                title="Șterge template">×</button>
        </button>`;
    }).join('');

    wrapper.style.display = 'block';
}

async function useTemplate(templateId) {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const chip = document.getElementById(`tmpl-${templateId}`);
    if (chip) chip.classList.add('loading');

    try {
        const url = `/food/templates/${templateId}/use?meal_type=${encodeURIComponent(selectedMealType)}`;
        const res  = await fetch(url, {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
        });
        const data = await res.json();

        if (!res.ok) {
            showToast(`⚠ ${data.detail || 'Nu s-a putut loga templateul.'}`, true);
            return;
        }

        const kcal = data.parsed?.totals?.calories || 0;
        showToast(`⚡ ${escapeHtml(data.template_name)} — ${kcal} kcal logat instant`);
        await loadFoodToday();
    } catch {
        showToast('⚠ Eroare de conexiune.', true);
    } finally {
        if (chip) chip.classList.remove('loading');
    }
}

async function deleteTemplate(templateId) {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    const chip = document.getElementById(`tmpl-${templateId}`);
    if (chip) { chip.style.opacity = '0.3'; chip.style.pointerEvents = 'none'; }

    try {
        const res = await fetch(`/food/templates/${templateId}`, {
            method:  'DELETE',
            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` },
        });
        if (res.ok) {
            showToast('✓ Template șters');
            await loadTemplates();
        } else {
            if (chip) { chip.style.opacity = ''; chip.style.pointerEvents = ''; }
            showToast('⚠ Nu s-a putut șterge.', true);
        }
    } catch {
        if (chip) { chip.style.opacity = ''; chip.style.pointerEvents = ''; }
    }
}

function showTemplateSuggest(suggestData) {
    _pendingTemplate = suggestData;

    const banner = document.getElementById('templateSuggestBanner');
    const descEl = document.getElementById('templateSuggestDesc');
    if (!banner) return;

    const preview = suggestData.name.length > 35
        ? suggestData.name.slice(0, 35) + '…'
        : suggestData.name;
    if (descEl) {
        descEl.textContent = `"${preview}" — un singur tap data viitoare, fără să mai scrii nimic.`;
    }

    banner.style.display = 'flex';

    if (_suggestAutoHide) clearTimeout(_suggestAutoHide);
    _suggestAutoHide = setTimeout(dismissTemplateSuggest, 15_000);
}

async function acceptTemplateSuggest() {
    if (!_pendingTemplate) return;

    const templateData = { ..._pendingTemplate };
    dismissTemplateSuggest();

    const token = localStorage.getItem('coaching_token');
    if (!token) return;

    try {
        const res = await fetch('/food/templates', {
            method:  'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type':  'application/json',
                'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(templateData),
        });
        const data = await res.json();

        if (res.ok) {
            showToast('⚡ Template salvat! Apasă-l data viitoare pentru logare instant.');
            await loadTemplates();
        } else {
            showToast(`⚠ ${data.detail || 'Eroare la salvarea template-ului.'}`, true);
        }
    } catch {
        showToast('⚠ Eroare de conexiune.', true);
    }
}

function dismissTemplateSuggest() {
    const banner = document.getElementById('templateSuggestBanner');
    if (banner) banner.style.display = 'none';
    if (_suggestAutoHide) { clearTimeout(_suggestAutoHide); _suggestAutoHide = null; }
    _pendingTemplate = null;
}   

// ═══ Avatar Upload System ══════════════════════════════════════════════
function avatarClick() {
  var existing = document.getElementById('avatarCtxMenu');
  if (existing) { existing.remove(); return; }
  var hasCustom = !!localStorage.getItem('nlAvatarData');
  var av = document.getElementById('acct-avatar');
  var rect = av.getBoundingClientRect();

  var menu = document.createElement('div');
  menu.id = 'avatarCtxMenu';
  menu.style.cssText =
    'position:fixed;z-index:950;background:#161616;' +
    'border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden;' +
    'min-width:190px;box-shadow:0 12px 48px rgba(0,0,0,0.7);' +
    'left:' + Math.min(rect.left, window.innerWidth - 210) + 'px;' +
    'top:' + (rect.bottom + 10) + 'px;';

  function menuItem(icon, label, action, danger) {
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.style.cssText =
      'display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;width:100%;padding:12px 16px;' +
      'background:transparent;border:none;cursor:pointer;font-family:Inter,sans-serif;' +
      'font-size:13px;font-weight:500;color:' + (danger ? '#ef4444' : '#d4d0cb') + ';' +
      'transition:background .15s;text-align:left;';
    btn.innerHTML = '<span>' + icon + '</span><span>' + label + '</span>';
    btn.onclick = function () { menu.remove(); action(); };
    btn.onmouseover = function () {
      btn.style.background = danger ? 'rgba(239,68,68,0.1)' : 'rgba(196,98,45,0.1)';
    };
    btn.onmouseout = function () { btn.style.background = 'transparent'; };
    return btn;
  }

  menu.appendChild(menuItem('📤', 'Încarcă poză', function () {
    document.getElementById('avatarFileInput').click();
  }, false));
  if (hasCustom) {
    menu.appendChild(menuItem('🗑️', 'Elimină poza', avatarRemove, true));
  }
  document.body.appendChild(menu);

  setTimeout(function () {
    function dismiss(e) {
      if (!menu.contains(e.target) && e.target !== av) {
        menu.remove();
        document.removeEventListener('click', dismiss);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('click', dismiss);
  }, 60);
}

function handleAvatarUpload(e) {
  var file = e.target.files[0];
  e.target.value = '';
  if (!file) return;
  if (file.size > 4 * 1024 * 1024) {
    alert('Poza trebuie să fie sub 4MB.');
    return;
  }
  var reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = function (ev) {
    // Comprimă via canvas
    var img = new Image();
    img.onload = function () {
      var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      var MAX = 256;
      var ratio = Math.min(MAX / img.width, MAX / img.height, 1);
      canvas.width = img.width * ratio;
      canvas.height = img.height * ratio;
      canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      var compressed = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.82);
      localStorage.setItem('nlAvatarData', compressed);
      avatarApply(compressed);
    };
    img.src = ev.target.result;
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
}

function avatarApply(src) {
  var img = document.getElementById('acct-avatar-img');
  var initials = document.getElementById('acct-avatar-initials');
  if (!img) return;
  img.src = src;
  img.style.display = 'block';
  if (initials) initials.style.visibility = 'hidden';
}

function avatarRemove() {
  localStorage.removeItem('nlAvatarData');
  var img = document.getElementById('acct-avatar-img');
  var initials = document.getElementById('acct-avatar-initials');
  if (img) { img.src = ''; img.style.display = 'none'; }
  if (initials) initials.style.visibility = 'visible';
}

// Hover overlay pe avatar
(function () {
  function setupHover() {
    var av = document.getElementById('acct-avatar');
    var ov = document.getElementById('acct-avatar-overlay');
    if (!av || !ov) return;
    av.addEventListener('mouseenter', function () { ov.style.display = 'flex'; });
    av.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () { ov.style.display = 'none'; });
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', setupHover);
  } else { setupHover(); }
})();

// Restaurează avatar la încărcare pagină
(function () {
  var saved = localStorage.getItem('nlAvatarData');
  if (!saved) return;
  function restore() { avatarApply(saved); }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', restore);
  } else { restore(); }
})();

</script>
